# 第6級第四批｜6A-13《神明集體罷工，人類開始選舉新神》

**When the Gods Went on Strike**

**類型：** 史詩政治奇幻  
**級距：** Level 6（5000–6000 字彙）  
**版本：** 拾頁 StorySteps｜第四批｜25 章完整教學初稿  
**正式配詞：** L6_A本_25章配詞.md，501 個原始 ID

## 故事簡介

十二位神明在王室葬禮清晨集體停止回應。選務書記發現每個奇蹟都從未同意的民眾身上扣費，宰相卻要求人民在冬天前選出第十三位神明。

**English premise:** Twelve gods stop answering prayers on the morning of a royal funeral. An election clerk discovers that every miracle was billed to someone who never consented, while the chancellor demands that voters elect a thirteenth god before winter.

## 世界規則

奇蹟從不會憑空創造能源或食物，只會把代價轉進帳簿，而且常未告知付費者。只有選民仍能說不，投票鐘才會計入選票。

**English:** A miracle never created energy or food from nothing; it moved a cost into a ledger, usually without telling the person who paid. The election bell counted a vote only when the person holding it could still say no.

## 閱讀方法與編輯說明

每一個 EN 英文段落後緊接繁中對照段落；詞彙證據句須出現在同章英文正文中。部分高階配詞取自本專案既有教學語料，依新題材整理，**尚未完成逐句文學編輯及語意終校**。本書為完整章節的教學初稿，並非出版定稿。第10章設有核心反轉，第25章主線收束。中文詞義依情境，不代表配詞原檔官方翻譯。

---

## CHAPTER 01｜The Funeral Without Rain｜沒有雨的葬禮

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：646**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The first sign that the gods were on strike was the queen’s dry coffin. Twelve thousand people waited in the rain court while priests lifted their arms toward an empty sky. The storm god had promised rain at every royal funeral for four centuries. Today the silver clouds remained motionless. Liora, chief clerk of the election archive, did not pray. She watched the bells. Eleven shook in the wind; the twelfth stayed stationary. When the priest tried to abbreviate the official prayer, a little boy in the front row shouted that the god was listening but refusing to answer. The whole crowd heard him.

**繁中** 眾神罷工的第一個徵兆，是女王的棺木上沒有一滴雨。一萬兩千人等在祈雨庭院，祭司向空蕩的天空舉起雙臂。四百年來，每逢王室葬禮，暴風神都承諾降雨。今天銀雲卻紋絲不動。選務檔案室主任莉歐拉沒有祈禱，只注意鐘聲。十一口鐘在風中晃動，只有第十二口靜止不動。祭司試著縮短正式禱文時，前排的小男孩喊道：神明有在聽，只是不願回答。所有人都聽見了。

**EN** The boy’s mother clasped him to her bosom. An orderly from the infirmary came to lead them away, but Liora asked the woman to stay. In her hand the mother held an invoice printed on virgin parchment, untouched by the rain that should have fallen. It charged her dead daughter for a miracle performed yesterday. Liora read the name twice. The child had been buried nine years earlier. Under payment, the form listed one year of memory. No law in the kingdom made such a charge applicable to the dead. Yet the state seal was genuine, and beneath it stood a warrant authorizing collection.

**繁中** 男孩的母親把他緊緊抱在胸前。醫務所的勤務員準備帶母子離開，莉歐拉卻請那位婦人留下。母親手中拿著印在全新羊皮紙上的帳單，本該落下的雨一滴也未沾濕紙面。帳單竟向她已死的女兒收取昨天奇蹟的費用。莉歐拉把名字讀了兩遍。那孩子九年前就下葬了。付款欄寫著「一年的記憶」。王國沒有法律允許向死者收這樣的費用，但國家印璽是真的，下方甚至有授權催收的令狀。

**EN** Outside the court, volunteers were broiling fish over coals for the mourners. A widow with a limp stood beside the clothing stall, where donated tunics, shawls, and a single bra hung on a rope. Everyone had brought something because the temple kitchens were closed. Liora looked beyond them to the old maritime anchor mounted at the junction of three streets. The anchor was supposed to keep the city’s miracle network grounded. If it had failed, the cost of every prayer might have gone somewhere no priest could see. But a comparative reading of the bronze meters showed a different possibility: nothing was broken. The network had been deliberately disconnected.

**繁中** 庭院外，志工在炭火上炙烤魚，分給參加葬禮的人。一名跛腳寡婦站在衣物攤旁，捐贈的上衣、披肩與一件胸罩掛在繩上。神殿廚房關閉後，人人都帶來能分享的東西。莉歐拉望向三條街交會處，舊海事船錨立在那裡。據說它負責穩定全城奇蹟網路。如果船錨故障，祈禱的代價可能被轉往祭司看不見的地方。然而，兩只銅錶的比較讀數顯示另一種可能：網路沒有壞，而是遭人主動切斷。

**EN** A bank teller came forward with a payment register. A gangster employed by a temple contractor had offered him a bribe to delete the names of families who could not pay their memory debts. The teller had refused; now he was afraid that speaking would put his children in danger. Liora told him she would not force him to narrate anything before a safe hearing could be arranged. His eyelid shook as he looked toward the guards. “The account is not only for miracles,” he whispered. “It includes election expenses.” That was the first time Liora understood why the royal council wanted a new god before winter.

**繁中** 銀行出納帶著付款紀錄走上前。一名受神殿承包商雇用的黑幫分子曾向他行賄，要求刪除付不起記憶債務的家庭姓名。出納拒絕了，如今卻害怕開口會危及孩子。莉歐拉告訴他，在安排安全聽證前不會強迫他敘述任何事。他望向守衛時眼皮發抖。「這個帳戶不只支付奇蹟，」他低聲說，「還包括選舉費用。」莉歐拉第一次明白，王室議會為何急著在冬天之前選出新神。

**EN** The funeral chamber opened again. Chancellor Varos entered carrying a ballot box, smiling as though the absence of rain were part of the ceremony. He declared that a new god would make the kingdom flourish and that all candidates must accept unconditional service. Liora asked where voters could register a refusal. Varos’s smile tightened. “The choice,” he replied, “is which god will protect you, not whether you will be protected.” Beside her, Nemi, a former temple singer, murmured that gods had once been allowed to resign. Liora had never heard that part of the hymn. She asked Nemi to sing it, but Nemi said the missing verse had been banned.

**繁中** 葬禮廳再次打開。瓦洛斯宰相抱著投票箱走進來，笑得彷彿沒有下雨也是儀式的一部分。他宣稱新神會使王國繁榮，而且每位候選者都必須接受無條件服務。莉歐拉問選民在哪裡登記反對。瓦洛斯的笑容僵住。「選項是由哪位神保護你，」他回答，「不是要不要受到保護。」一旁的前神殿歌者妮米輕聲說，神明過去其實可以辭職。莉歐拉從沒在聖歌裡聽過這件事。她請妮米唱出來，妮米卻說缺失的那一段早已遭禁。

**EN** When the votes were laid out, Liora noticed that the names of several candidates had already been printed on the paper before nominations opened. The time available to investigate was finite: Varos intended to close registration that very evening. She slid the dead child’s invoice between two pages of the election register and demanded access to the original account book. For a moment she expected the guards to arrest her. Instead, the twelfth bell rang once, without a hand touching it. Everyone turned toward the sound. Under the ballot box, in lettering older than the kingdom itself, a sentence appeared: WE HAVE NOT ABANDONED YOU. WE HAVE REFUSED TO STEAL FROM YOU.

**繁中** 莉歐拉攤開選票時，發現提名尚未開始，紙上便已印好幾名候選人的名字。她能調查的時間很有限：瓦洛斯打算當晚就關閉登記。她把死去孩子的帳單夾進選舉紀錄，要求調閱原始帳簿。那一刻她以為守衛會逮捕自己。沒想到第十二口鐘在無人碰觸下響了一聲。所有人轉頭望去。投票箱底下浮出比王國更古老的文字：「我們沒有拋棄你們。我們拒絕再從你們身上偷取代價。」

### 理解測驗

1. **What important problem does the opening scene reveal?**
   - A. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - B. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - C. During the queen’s funeral, the storm god refuses to send even one drop, and election clerk Liora finds a bill addressed to a dead child.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 女王葬禮上，雨神連一滴雨都不肯降下；選務書記莉歐拉卻發現一張寄給死去孩子的帳單。

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. The invoice names a miracle the child never requested, and its payment is recorded as one year of memory.
   - B. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - C. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 帳單記載孩子從未祈求的奇蹟，付款方式竟是整整一年的記憶。


## CHAPTER 02｜Twelve Empty Altars｜十二座空祭壇

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：887**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** A messenger arrived before the bells had finished ringing. The news sounded impossible only because everyone had been trained not to question the institution that delivered it. Liora visits each temple and discovers identical notices announcing a strike with no explanation of the gods’ demands. Liora asked the messenger to repeat the part that had been omitted from the written notice. It was there, in the omission, that the day's real story began.

**繁中** 鐘聲尚未結束，信使已經抵達。消息之所以聽來不可能，只因每個人都習慣不質疑傳遞消息的制度。莉歐拉走訪每座神殿，發現十二張完全相同的罷工公告，卻沒有任何神明提出要求。莉歐拉請信使重述書面公告遺漏的部分。這一天真正的故事，就從那處缺漏開始。

**EN** A miracle never created energy or food from nothing; it moved a cost into a ledger, usually without telling the person who paid. The election bell counted a vote only when the person holding it could still say no.

**繁中** 奇蹟從不會憑空創造能源或食物，只會把代價轉進帳簿，而且常未告知付費者。只有選民仍能說不，投票鐘才會計入選票。

**EN** There were children behind the temple steps who could remember the taste of bread but not the people who had taught them to bake it. Liora did not mistake their silence for gratitude. Every miracle that filled a bowl and emptied a memory left a different kind of hunger behind.

**繁中** 神殿階梯後方的孩子記得麵包滋味，卻不記得教自己烤麵包的人。莉歐拉沒有把沉默誤認為感激。每場填飽碗、卻掏空記憶的奇蹟，都會留下另一種飢餓。

**EN** The evidence did not arrive as a confession. It came through a date that did not fit, a measurement that had been repeated, and a witness who knew exactly what the official account had left out. Liora asked Nemi not to describe suspicion as proof. If the accusation was wrong, an innocent person would suffer. If it was right, careless language could still give the guilty a way to escape.

**繁中** 證據不是以自白形式出現，而是一個對不上的日期、一項重複測量的結果，以及一位知道官方說法遺漏何事的證人。莉歐拉請妮米不要把懷疑說成證明。若指控錯了，無辜者會受害；若指控正確，草率措辭仍可能讓有罪者脫身。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: An aboriginal community upstream requested access to the substation's emergency warning system. Small settlements abound along the ridges, where families tended terraced gardens under extreme weather. Liora tried to align their route with the royal charts, but the marked crossing had been destroyed months earlier. At that altitude the air was thin enough to make breathing difficult. The magistrate concealed his annoyance when Iva asked him to repeat a legal question slowly. Nemi marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 上游原住民族社群要求能接收到變電站的緊急警報。 山脊遍布小聚落，家家在嚴酷天候下照顧梯田。 莉歐拉試著讓路線與王室地圖對齊，卻發現標示的渡口早在數月前就毀了。 這樣的海拔使空氣稀薄，呼吸格外費力。 伊娃請地方法官慢慢重述法律問題時，他掩飾住自己的不耐。 妮米標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A small act of honesty unsettled the room more than a shouted threat could have done. Nemi, a former temple singer, recognizes the notices as copies printed before the gods disappeared. Liora watched a witness decide not to change a difficult detail merely because it complicated the preferred story. Nemi recorded the correction openly. Their inquiry would be slower now, but its shape belonged less to the powerful and more to the people who had lived through the event.

**繁中** 一個小小的誠實舉動，帶來比喊叫威脅更強的震動。前神殿歌者妮米認出，這些公告早在神明消失前就已印製。莉歐拉看見證人沒有因為細節讓主流說法變複雜就改口。妮米公開記錄修正。調查會因此變慢，卻能較少受權貴支配，也更貼近親歷者。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: Iva gave a blunt answer: her neighbors had agreed to store grain, never to lose their homes. The clerk entered crossing in the testimony without deciding what it proved. Liora refused to discard an outdated map while its original annotations remained unexamined. The harbor discharge record showed when the packed cases had left the boat. Their joint endeavor would be assessed through measurements, not a flattering press release. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 伊娃直截了當地回答：鄰居同意保管糧食，從未同意失去家園。 書記將「crossing」記入證詞，沒有立即判定它能證明什麼。 原始註解還沒查清，瑪拉拒絕丟棄舊地圖。 港口卸貨紀錄寫明包裝展箱何時離船。 雙方共同努力將以測量結果評估，而非以討好的新聞稿。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** No one in the room was permitted to declare that a good intention canceled a harmful action. Liora insisted on recording the error, identifying who had authorized it, and protecting those affected before assigning wider blame. Nemi drew a simple boundary around what they could do tonight. The line looked disappointingly modest, yet it was the first promise that did not require another person to surrender a right.

**繁中** 室內沒有人能宣稱善意可以抵銷傷害。莉歐拉堅持記錄錯誤、確認授權者，並在追究更大責任前保護受害者。妮米畫出今晚能做到的清楚界線。這條線看似微不足道，卻是第一個不要求別人放棄權利的承諾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The first hurdle was getting both companies to accept a technician chosen by workers. Lira changed her posture when the resident began speaking, listening without interruption. The interpreter demanded precision in the word for custody because a careless synonym could transfer ownership. The officer announced that travelers must undergo rigorous questioning, and that no royal family member could veto the security rules. At a wind-battered terrace, the party found an elderly widow repairing her roof. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 第一道障礙是讓兩家公司接受工人推選的技師。 居民開始發言時，莉拉調整姿勢，沒有打斷對方。 譯員要求精確使用「保管」一詞，因為草率的同義詞可能轉移所有權。 軍官宣布旅人都須接受嚴格盤查，任何王室成員也不能否決安全規則。 在飽受狂風吹襲的梯田邊，隊伍找到一名修屋頂的年長寡婦。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** There were children behind the temple steps who could remember the taste of bread but not the people who had taught them to bake it. Liora did not mistake their silence for gratitude. Every miracle that filled a bowl and emptied a memory left a different kind of hunger behind.

**繁中** 神殿階梯後方的孩子記得麵包滋味，卻不記得教自己烤麵包的人。莉歐拉沒有把沉默誤認為感激。每場填飽碗、卻掏空記憶的奇蹟，都會留下另一種飢餓。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: A workers' uprising was not the issue; they were requesting the safety rights already promised. A volunteer usher led witnesses through the side entrance to protect their privacy. The versatile backup unit could power either a clinic refrigerator or an emergency radio. The local council chose not to veto the temporary food route merely because the palace disliked it. The widow refused payment but asked that her testimony be carried to an independent court. Liora asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 這不是工人起義；他們只是要求已被承諾的安全權利。 志工引導員帶證人從側門進入，以保護隱私。 多功能備用裝置可供應診所冰箱或緊急無線電。 地方議會沒有只因王宮不喜歡就否決臨時糧食路線。 寡婦拒絕收錢，只求她把證詞交給獨立法庭。 莉歐拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** On the way out, Liora passed people waiting for a result that would affect their meals, their homes, or their ability to say no. None of them had the luxury of treating the dispute as an intellectual puzzle. Nemi paused to answer a child's practical question before they left. The answer was incomplete, but unlike the old official notice, it admitted the uncertainty out loud.

**繁中** 離開時，莉歐拉經過等待結果的人群，這項結果將影響他們的餐食、家園或說不的能力。他們不能奢侈地把爭論當成智力謎題。妮米停下回答一名孩子實際的問題。答案並不完整，卻與舊公告不同：它坦白承認不確定之處。

### 本章新目標詞

### 理解測驗

1. **Why does the first event matter?**
   - A. Liora visits each temple and discovers identical notices announcing a strike with no explanation of the gods’ demands.
   - B. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - C. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 莉歐拉走訪每座神殿，發現十二張完全相同的罷工公告，卻沒有任何神明提出要求。

2. **Which finding changes the characters’ understanding?**
   - A. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   - B. Nemi, a former temple singer, recognizes the notices as copies printed before the gods disappeared.
   - C. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 前神殿歌者妮米認出，這些公告早在神明消失前就已印製。


## CHAPTER 03｜A Vote Before Winter｜入冬前的投票

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：868**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The first sign of trouble was not an explosion or a scream. It was a familiar procedure producing a result nobody could defend. Chancellor Varos announces a public election for the next god, promising warmth to every district that registers early. Liora checked the time, the witnesses, and the rule that authorized the action. The three did not agree. Outside, ordinary life continued, which made the error more frightening rather than less.

**繁中** 麻煩的第一個徵兆不是爆炸或尖叫，而是熟悉程序得出了誰也無法辯護的結果。瓦洛斯宰相宣布舉行新神選舉，承諾凡提前登記的街區都會得到暖氣。莉歐拉核對時間、證人與授權規則，三者卻互相矛盾。外頭的平凡生活照常進行，反而讓這個錯誤更加可怕。

**EN** A miracle never created energy or food from nothing; it moved a cost into a ledger, usually without telling the person who paid. The election bell counted a vote only when the person holding it could still say no.

**繁中** 奇蹟從不會憑空創造能源或食物，只會把代價轉進帳簿，而且常未告知付費者。只有選民仍能說不，投票鐘才會計入選票。

**EN** There were children behind the temple steps who could remember the taste of bread but not the people who had taught them to bake it. Liora did not mistake their silence for gratitude. Every miracle that filled a bowl and emptied a memory left a different kind of hunger behind.

**繁中** 神殿階梯後方的孩子記得麵包滋味，卻不記得教自己烤麵包的人。莉歐拉沒有把沉默誤認為感激。每場填飽碗、卻掏空記憶的奇蹟，都會留下另一種飢餓。

**EN** Beyond the meeting room, people were already finding ways to survive. They traded tools, shared the location of safe passages, and argued over fair turns without waiting for permission. Liora listened longer than the schedule allowed. Nemi reminded the officials that no policy was complete until it made sense to the people expected to live with it. Their notes began with those ordinary acts of cooperation.

**繁中** 會議室外，人們早已開始尋求生存方法：交換工具、分享安全路線的位置，無須等候許可便爭論公平輪班。莉歐拉聽得比議程允許的更久。妮米提醒官員，政策若不能讓實際生活其中的人理解，就不算完整。他們從這些平凡合作開始記錄。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: An advisory plaque still praised the Empire's conquest of the eastern frontier, but most houses were empty. A mural celebrated royal conquest, but the community diary described families driven from their homes. Lira searched for evidence of a conspiracy rather than assume it. Someone had left a cucumber sandwich beside the abandoned recording equipment. The portable radio emitted a feeble signal from the edge of the substation. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 告示牌仍讚揚帝國征服東部邊境，但大多數房屋已空無一人。 壁畫歌頌王室征服，社群日記卻記錄被迫離家的家庭。 莉拉沒有先認定陰謀存在，而是尋找證據。 有人在廢置錄音設備旁留下一份小黃瓜三明治。 便攜無線電從變電站邊緣發出微弱訊號。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** Then the next piece of evidence changed the shape of the case. An unregistered quarter receives heat anyway, proving the promise is not a law of nature. Liora read the statement twice, searching for the sentence that would make the contradiction disappear. There was none. Nemi did not reach for a dramatic accusation; instead, they listed three people who could verify the finding separately. The delay would be unpopular, but a dramatic discovery deserved more care, not less.

**繁中** 接著出現的新證據改變了整起案件的輪廓。未登記的街區卻仍然得到熱能，證明宰相的承諾不是不可改變的自然法則。莉歐拉把陳述讀了兩遍，試圖找出能消除矛盾的句子，卻沒有找到。妮米沒有立刻提出轟動指控，而是列出三位可分別驗證發現的人。延遲會不受歡迎，但重大發現更需要謹慎。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: A firecracker exploded outside the gate, and the officers checked whether it was a signal or prank. The mechanic began to fume when his accurate warning was described as a rumor. The delivery map was stained with grease from the night-shift kitchen. A careful inspection might lessen the danger to villages still connected to the hidden pipes. An abandoned river liner stood at the dock, its passenger deck still painted with cheerful blue stars. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 門外一枚爆竹炸響，官員檢查究竟是暗號還是惡作劇。 技師見自己的準確警告被說成謠言，開始氣憤。 送貨地圖沾有夜班廚房的油脂。 仔細檢查或許能減輕仍連著暗管的村莊所面臨的危險。 一艘廢棄的河上客輪停在碼頭，乘客甲板上仍畫著開朗的藍色星星。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The debate sharpened when Chancellor Varos demanded a decision before the evidence could be copied. Liora replied that urgency and secrecy were not the same thing. They established a temporary limit, published the reason for it, and scheduled an independent review. The result satisfied nobody completely. That was acceptable; the purpose of the process was to make abuse harder, not to produce applause.

**繁中** 當瓦洛斯宰相要求在證據複製前就做決定，辯論變得尖銳。莉歐拉回應說緊急和保密不是同一回事。團隊訂立暫時限制、公開理由，並排定獨立審查。結果沒有讓任何人完全滿意，但程序的目的本來就不是得到掌聲，而是使濫權更困難。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The old orphanage had been promised funding that was never entered in its bank account. Residents compared the recurring blackouts to a plague that never seemed to end. The community requested recreational lighting for its sports field only after clinics were safe. The video editor warned that online debate had become savage toward the unknown driver. A broken swing moved in the wind beside a shrub growing through the steps. Liora asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 舊孤兒院被承諾的經費從未存入銀行帳戶。 居民把反覆神殿沉默比作似乎永不結束的災難。 社區表示診所安全後，再考慮運動場休閒照明。 影像編輯警告網路討論對不明司機已變得殘酷。 破損的鞦韆在風中搖動，階梯縫裡長出灌木。 莉歐拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** There were children behind the temple steps who could remember the taste of bread but not the people who had taught them to bake it. Liora did not mistake their silence for gratitude. Every miracle that filled a bowl and emptied a memory left a different kind of hunger behind.

**繁中** 神殿階梯後方的孩子記得麵包滋味，卻不記得教自己烤麵包的人。莉歐拉沒有把沉默誤認為感激。每場填飽碗、卻掏空記憶的奇蹟，都會留下另一種飢餓。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The technician explained that a local slang term had been mistaken for an equipment code. The second camera could surpass the first in clarity without identifying the people shown. For workers the damaged seal came to symbolize years of ignored safety warnings. A textile pattern on the borrowed coat helped establish when it had been photographed. The clinic kept its vaccine refrigerator running while the citywide evidence review continued. Nemi marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 技師解釋某個地方俚語被誤認成設備代碼。 第二台攝影機雖然清晰度勝過第一台，仍無法辨認人物。 對工人而言，破損封條象徵多年被忽略的安全警告。 外借大衣的紡織圖案協助確認它何時被拍攝。 全市證據審查期間，診所仍維持疫苗冰箱運作。 妮米標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A message arrived after the public doors had closed. It carried no grand declaration, just a name and a time that did not match the official schedule. Liora resisted the urge to call it proof. Nemi opened a fresh page in the journal and recorded the discrepancy exactly. They had won time, not certainty, and there were still people who might lose everything if they hurried.

**繁中** 公共大門關閉後，一則訊息送到。它沒有豪言壯語，只有一個名字和一個對不上官方時程的時間。莉歐拉克制住把它當成證明的衝動。妮米翻開日誌新的一頁，逐字記錄差異。他們贏得的是時間，不是確定性；若急於求成，仍有人可能失去一切。

### 理解測驗

1. **What unexpected event begins this chapter?**
   - A. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - B. Chancellor Varos announces a public election for the next god, promising warmth to every district that registers early.
   - C. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 瓦洛斯宰相宣布舉行新神選舉，承諾凡提前登記的街區都會得到暖氣。

2. **What does the later discovery establish?**
   - A. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - B. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - C. An unregistered quarter receives heat anyway, proving the promise is not a law of nature.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 未登記的街區卻仍然得到熱能，證明宰相的承諾不是不可改變的自然法則。


## CHAPTER 04｜The Candidate Who Cannot Heal｜不會治癒的候選人

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：845**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** Morning brought a decision that had already been made for everyone. There was a signature at the bottom of the order, but no space for those most affected to answer. A celebrated healer enters the race, but Liora finds the names of patients whose recovery was secretly paid for by strangers. Liora knew that refusing a bad choice was not the same as knowing the correct one. Before making a promise, the team needed something they could test.

**繁中** 清晨帶來一項替所有人做好的決定。命令底下有簽名，卻沒有讓受影響最深者回答的地方。名醫宣布參選，莉歐拉卻找到病人康復所需代價被轉嫁給陌生人的名單。莉歐拉知道拒絕壞選項不等於已經知道正確答案。作出承諾前，團隊需要能夠驗證的事物。

**EN** There were children behind the temple steps who could remember the taste of bread but not the people who had taught them to bake it. Liora did not mistake their silence for gratitude. Every miracle that filled a bowl and emptied a memory left a different kind of hunger behind.

**繁中** 神殿階梯後方的孩子記得麵包滋味，卻不記得教自己烤麵包的人。莉歐拉沒有把沉默誤認為感激。每場填飽碗、卻掏空記憶的奇蹟，都會留下另一種飢餓。

**EN** Nemi arrived with a second account that neither confirmed nor dismissed the first. They compared the routes by which each record had reached the room, then placed the uncertainties beside the facts. Liora could feel the pressure to announce a solution before anyone became frightened. That pressure was precisely why a second witness mattered: a conclusion that could not survive an independent question would be dangerous, even if it happened to be right.

**繁中** 妮米帶來第二份說法，既未證實也未推翻第一份。兩人比對紀錄抵達房間的途徑，再把不確定之處和事實並列。莉歐拉感受到眾人希望立即宣布解方，以免恐懼擴大的壓力。但也正因這種壓力，第二位證人才重要：無法通過獨立質問的結論，即使碰巧正確，也可能很危險。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The cleaning team used bleach only after investigators recorded the stains near the door. The union requested a comprehensive list of every visitor with access to the storage area. A credible witness described what she heard but refused to guess who issued the command. Lira praised the workers' dedication to preserving damaged equipment instead of concealing it. The guest room held an old dresser and a thermometer with a cracked glass stem. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 調查員記錄門旁污漬後，清潔隊才使用漂白水。 工會要求完整列出所有能進入儲藏區的訪客。 可信的證人描述聽到的話，拒絕猜測誰下命令。 莉拉讚賞工人保存損壞設備而非藏匿的投入。 客房裡有一座舊五斗櫃，以及玻璃管裂開的溫度計。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The contradiction reached the table just as the meeting was about to end. The healer offers to quit if the real cost is released; Varos threatens to close the hospitals. For a long moment no one spoke. Liora understood why silence felt comfortable; each answer threatened someone's settled account of how the world worked. Nemi broke the stillness by asking who would be harmed if they simply filed the discovery away. No one could answer without naming a real person.

**繁中** 會議即將結束時，矛盾證據送上桌面。名醫願意在代價公開後退出，瓦洛斯卻以關閉醫院威脅眾人。一時之間無人開口。莉歐拉明白沉默為何讓人舒服：任何答案都可能動搖某人對世界的既定理解。妮米打破沉默，問若把發現歸檔，究竟誰會受害。沒人能在不指名真實人物的情況下回答。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: The guard's familiarity with the corridor helped reconstruct the route taken that night. The office hierarchy did not give the chairman permission to disregard witness protection rules. The landlady of the nearby boarding house remembered an unusual delivery before dawn. Beyond it, the marsh looked lush and peaceful, concealing collapsed drainage channels. The villagers still mourn the ferryman lost during last year's unsafe winter crossing. Liora asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 警衛對走廊的熟悉協助重建當晚路徑。 辦公室階級制度不允許董事長無視證人保護規則。 附近寄宿屋的女房東記得黎明前一件異常送貨。 更遠處的沼澤看似草木茂盛而平靜，實際卻藏著崩毀的排水道。 村民仍哀悼去年冬季危險渡口事故中失去的渡船夫。 莉歐拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Liora had expected the hardest part to be exposing the secret. Instead, it was choosing what to do after a partial truth became public. The people who had been ignored did not all want the same remedy. Nemi proposed that each affected group could submit a written objection and receive a specific answer. The method would take time, but it recognized them as participants rather than scenery.

**繁中** 莉歐拉原以為最難的是揭露祕密，後來才發現，部分真相公開後如何行動才是難題。曾被忽略的人並不全想要相同補救。妮米提議每個受影響群體都能提出書面異議並取得具體答覆。方法需要時間，但終於把他們當成參與者，而不是背景。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The teacher hoped a fair treaty would nurture trust among children speaking different languages. Rain pressed against the windows, and the river threatened to overflow its banks. A cleaner had begun to overhear the order before the supervisor closed the door. The company's new report could refute a rumor only by presenting verifiable records. A gull circled the quay where a long-abandoned concert hall once hosted a symphony every spring. Nemi marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 老師希望公平條約能培養使用不同語言的孩子間的信任。 雨水敲打窗戶，河水幾乎溢出河岸。 主管關門前，清潔員已無意間聽到那項命令。 公司新報告只能用可驗證紀錄反駁謠言。 一隻海鷗盤旋在碼頭上，附近那座廢棄音樂廳從前每年春天都會演奏交響樂。 妮米標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** There were children behind the temple steps who could remember the taste of bread but not the people who had taught them to bake it. Liora did not mistake their silence for gratitude. Every miracle that filled a bowl and emptied a memory left a different kind of hunger behind.

**繁中** 神殿階梯後方的孩子記得麵包滋味，卻不記得教自己烤麵包的人。莉歐拉沒有把沉默誤認為感激。每場填飽碗、卻掏空記憶的奇蹟，都會留下另一種飢餓。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The site superintendent admitted that he had approved a broken gate's temporary use. The clerk entered symphony in the testimony without deciding what it proved. The infirmary nurse placed a thermometer beside an ill courier and offered him rest before questioning. Lira said transparent testimony mattered more than turning the hearing into public turmoil. Volunteers prepared wholesome meals for interviewees who had missed the last bus. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 現場總監承認自己曾批准暫時使用損壞閘門。 書記將「symphony」記入證詞，沒有立即判定它能證明什麼。 醫務室護士把溫度計放在生病信差旁，讓他先休息再接受詢問。 莉拉說透明證詞比把聽證變成公眾騷動更重要。 志工為錯過末班車的受訪者準備營養均衡的餐點。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** When the lamps were lowered, Liora returned to a cracked voting bell. It was neither proof of innocence nor a shortcut to justice. It was a reminder that the next decision would be witnessed by people who no longer trusted easy promises. Nemi remained at the door while the final record was sealed. Outside, the twelve-bell capital waited for news that had to be more precise than a victory speech.

**繁中** 燈光調暗時，莉歐拉再次看向一口裂開的投票鐘。它既不是清白證明，也不是通往正義的捷徑，而是在提醒眾人：下一個決定會由不再相信輕易承諾的人見證。妮米守在門邊，最後一份紀錄被封存。外頭的十二鐘都等待的消息，必須比勝利演說更精確。

### 理解測驗

1. **What important problem does the opening scene reveal?**
   - A. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - B. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - C. A celebrated healer enters the race, but Liora finds the names of patients whose recovery was secretly paid for by strangers.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 名醫宣布參選，莉歐拉卻找到病人康復所需代價被轉嫁給陌生人的名單。

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. The healer offers to quit if the real cost is released; Varos threatens to close the hospitals.
   - B. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   - C. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 名醫願意在代價公開後退出，瓦洛斯卻以關閉醫院威脅眾人。


## CHAPTER 05｜The Thirteenth Ballot｜第十三張選票

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：854**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** By noon, rumors had outrun the facts. Some people wanted a villain; others wanted a hero who could end the argument without asking another question. Nemi finds an obsolete ballot design in which the thirteenth choice means none of the gods rather than another ruler. Liora had worked long enough to distrust both shortcuts. In the twelve-bell capital, the first task was to protect the evidence before anyone could turn it into a weapon.

**繁中** 到了中午，謠言早已跑得比事實快。有人想找反派，也有人想找英雄，好在不再追問的情況下結束爭論。妮米找到舊版選票，上面的第十三個選項不是新統治者，而是「不選任何神明」。莉歐拉工作多年，已經不相信這兩種捷徑。在十二鐘都，當務之急是先保護證據，免得任何人將它變成武器。

**EN** There were children behind the temple steps who could remember the taste of bread but not the people who had taught them to bake it. Liora did not mistake their silence for gratitude. Every miracle that filled a bowl and emptied a memory left a different kind of hunger behind.

**繁中** 神殿階梯後方的孩子記得麵包滋味，卻不記得教自己烤麵包的人。莉歐拉沒有把沉默誤認為感激。每場填飽碗、卻掏空記憶的奇蹟，都會留下另一種飢餓。

**EN** The argument was not merely about who held authority. It was about what happened when an official decision met a household, a workplace, or a body that could not obey it safely. Liora asked Nemi to describe the smallest practical consequence. Only then did they realize that the proposed remedy would reproduce the very harm it claimed to prevent. They changed the plan before presenting it in public.

**繁中** 爭論不只是誰有權威，而是官方決定落到家庭、職場或無法安全服從的身體上時會發生什麼。莉歐拉請妮米說明最小、最實際的後果。他們這才發現提議中的補救會重複它聲稱要防止的傷害，因此在公開發表前修改了計畫。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The company academy offered a practical course on emergency power rather than executive slogans. A false accusation against the warehouse team was withdrawn after the access records were checked. A banquet was taking place across the courtyard, where donors praised the war effort. A relief grant originally intended for fertilizer purchases had been redirected without explanation. The riverside fishery requested a dedicated backup line to protect its refrigerated catch. Liora asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 公司學院開設緊急供電實務課，而非宣傳主管口號。 查核門禁紀錄後，對倉庫團隊的不實指控遭撤回。 庭院對面正在舉行宴會，捐助者讚美戰爭。 原訂購買肥料的救助補助遭不明轉用。 河岸漁業要求專用備電線路，以保護冷藏漁獲。 莉歐拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** What changed the discussion was a fact that should have been obvious from the start. Someone has scraped that option off the public printing plates. Liora felt the old temptation to turn that fact into an immediate victory. Instead, the team separated what the new evidence established from what it merely suggested. That distinction frustrated the loudest spectators, but it helped the witnesses remain willing to speak again.

**繁中** 改變討論的是一件本來就該看得見的事實。有人把這個選項從公開印版上刮掉。莉歐拉一瞬間也想把它當成立刻勝利的證明，團隊卻把新證據真正確立的部分與僅能推測的部分分開。這讓最喧鬧的旁觀者失望，卻使證人願意再次出面。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: The inspection revealed a small fracture in the connector supporting the temporary generator. Technicians learned to harness surplus power without bypassing the required safety checks. Each operator wore headphones to hear control-room instructions over the storm warnings. The clinic reviewed its oxygen intake records to calculate emergency battery demand. In the courtyard, a troupe rehearsed a ceremonial dance called the Maiden's Promise, though its lyrics had been rewritten to glorify the war. Nemi marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 檢查發現支撐臨時發電機的接頭有一道小裂縫。 技師學習利用剩餘電力，不繞過必要安全檢查。 每位操作員戴耳機，以在風暴警報聲中聽清控制室指示。 診所檢視氧氣攝入紀錄，計算緊急電池需求。 庭院裡，一個劇團排練名為〈少女的承諾〉的儀式舞蹈，但歌詞早被改成頌揚戰爭。 妮米標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** There were two tempting shortcuts. One would protect the reputation of Chancellor Varos and leave the public guessing. The other would expose every private detail at once and call that transparency. Liora rejected both. The issue was whether a miracle may be taken from a citizen without consent, not whether the team could win an argument in a single afternoon. A smaller step, supported by independent observers, could protect people while the wider dispute remained open.

**繁中** 有兩種誘人的捷徑：一種保住瓦洛斯宰相的名聲，讓大眾自行猜測；另一種把所有私人細節一次揭露，並稱之為透明。莉歐拉兩者都拒絕。核心是奇蹟能否未經同意就向公民索取代價，不是團隊能否在一個下午贏得爭論。由獨立觀察者支持的一小步，即使爭議未結，也能先保護民眾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: A young apprentice described an accusation against Daven: he had supposedly stolen a design for offshore heat collectors from the academy. Now she allowed them to enter a sealed lecture hall, but only as prospective witnesses, not celebrated heroes. A resident asked for a referendum on the long-term public ownership of the canal land. Daven invited Lira into the old workshop where they had learned to refine copper anchors. The nurses could finally rejoice when the new clinic batteries passed their first test. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 一名年輕學徒說，達文據稱偷走學院的離岸熱能採集器設計。 如今她允許他們進入封閉講堂，但只能以潛在證人身分入內，不能當成受歡迎的英雄。 一名居民要求針對運河土地的長期公共所有權舉辦公投。 達文帶莉拉進入舊工坊，他們以前在這裡學習精煉銅製媒介。 新診所電池通過首次測試後，護理師終於能高興起來。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** There were children behind the temple steps who could remember the taste of bread but not the people who had taught them to bake it. Liora did not mistake their silence for gratitude. Every miracle that filled a bowl and emptied a memory left a different kind of hunger behind.

**繁中** 神殿階梯後方的孩子記得麵包滋味，卻不記得教自己烤麵包的人。莉歐拉沒有把沉默誤認為感激。每場填飽碗、卻掏空記憶的奇蹟，都會留下另一種飢餓。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: Some shareholders began to resent the safety veto, calling it an obstacle to profit. The operating room remained serene after its generator switched smoothly to backup power. A neighborhood pastor gave a short sermon about caring for people before property. The board refused to shuffle expenses between accounts to make the trial look cheaper. A singer borrowed Lira's scarf and explained that her tenant family had been evicted when army engineers demanded their land. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 部分股東開始怨恨安全否決權，稱它阻礙獲利。 手術室的發電機順利切換備電後，室內依舊平靜。 社區牧師簡短講道，提醒人們應先照顧人，再談財產。 董事會拒絕在帳戶間調換支出，營造試辦更便宜的假象。 一位歌手借了莉拉的圍巾，說自己的租戶家庭因軍方工程師徵地而遭驅逐。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The chapter ended without an easy reassurance. Liora had learned one thing that could be checked and another that still needed an answer. Nemi wrote both on the same page so that relief would not erase doubt. Their decision had moved the crisis forward, but it had also drawn the attention of Chancellor Varos. By morning, the next question would reach people far beyond this room.

**繁中** 這一天沒有簡單的安慰。莉歐拉知道了一件能查證的事，也留下另一件尚待回答的事。妮米把兩者寫在同一頁，免得鬆一口氣就抹掉疑問。他們的決定使危機向前，也引起瓦洛斯宰相注意。明早下一個問題將傳到房間外更遠的地方。

### 理解測驗

1. **Why does the first event matter?**
   - A. Nemi finds an obsolete ballot design in which the thirteenth choice means none of the gods rather than another ruler.
   - B. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - C. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 妮米找到舊版選票，上面的第十三個選項不是新統治者，而是「不選任何神明」。

2. **Which finding changes the characters’ understanding?**
   - A. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - B. Someone has scraped that option off the public printing plates.
   - C. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 有人把這個選項從公開印版上刮掉。


## CHAPTER 06｜Bread That Costs a Birthday｜一塊麵包的生日代價

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：837**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The room smelled of dust, hot metal, and rain carried in on people's coats. Someone had left an ordinary object on the central table, yet no one wanted to touch it. Liora traces the bread miracles to a ledger that subtracts birthdays from families during the winter famine. Liora saw that the object mattered less for what it was than for the decision it proved had been made. There would be no honest way back to ignorance.

**繁中** 室內有灰塵、熱金屬與人們外套帶進的雨味。有人把平凡物件放在中央桌上，卻沒有人願意碰它。莉歐拉追查冬季飢荒的麵包奇蹟，發現帳簿把家庭成員的生日記憶扣走。莉歐拉明白重要的不是物件本身，而是它證明了何種決定已經發生。如今已不可能誠實地假裝無知。

**EN** There were children behind the temple steps who could remember the taste of bread but not the people who had taught them to bake it. Liora did not mistake their silence for gratitude. Every miracle that filled a bowl and emptied a memory left a different kind of hunger behind.

**繁中** 神殿階梯後方的孩子記得麵包滋味，卻不記得教自己烤麵包的人。莉歐拉沒有把沉默誤認為感激。每場填飽碗、卻掏空記憶的奇蹟，都會留下另一種飢餓。

**EN** A witness asked whether the inquiry would make tomorrow safer. Liora could not promise that. The truth could establish responsibility, but it would not repair a broken machine, feed a hungry child, or return lost time on its own. Nemi suggested a temporary protection that could be checked after sunrise. They wrote down its limits in plain language and invited the people affected to challenge them.

**繁中** 一位證人問調查是否能讓明天更安全，莉歐拉無法保證。真相能確立責任，卻不會自行修好機器、餵飽孩子或歸還失去的時間。妮米提出一項日出後可以檢驗的暫時保護措施。他們用白話寫清限制，邀請受影響的人提出異議。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: Lira continued to allege that the entire investigation was a publicity stunt. The contractor had promised to beautify the riverside warehouse before its safety inspection. Maelin traced the caption to a committee meeting where no qualified river interpreter had been present. The contract dispute reached its climax when the two shipping weights appeared together. Lira asked the auditor to extract the original invoice data from the server. Nemi marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 莉拉繼續聲稱整場調查只是公關表演。 承包商曾承諾在安檢前美化河岸倉庫。 莉歐拉查出圖說來自一場沒有合格河語口譯員出席的委員會議。 當兩張出貨重量表一起出現時，契約爭議達到高潮。 莉拉要求稽核員從伺服器擷取原始發票資料。 妮米標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** For once the numbers did not merely decorate a report; they contradicted the order that had been issued in their name. A baker remembers kneading dough beside a daughter but can no longer recall her face. Nemi asked whether a mistake could explain the result. Liora agreed to test that possibility too. They checked an independent source, marked every assumption, and made copies before the originals could be transferred to an office beyond public reach.

**繁中** 這一次數字不只是報告裝飾，而是直接反駁以數字名義發布的命令。麵包師記得和女兒一起揉麵團，卻再也想不起女兒的臉。妮米詢問是否可能只是出錯，莉歐拉也同意檢查這種可能。他們核對獨立來源、標記每項假設，在原件被移往公眾無法接觸的辦公室前留下副本。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: A delegation of academy students had arrived after an extracurricular debate class. Their teacher was hospitable, offering water and a shady bench to witnesses who had traveled far. The usually jolly driver grew quiet when he recognized the private auction seal. Workers offered to mow the neglected path so elderly witnesses could reach the hall. The clerk, a cheerful man with a large mustache, agreed to write the words exactly as spoken. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 一群學院學生參加課外辯論課後來到法庭。 他們的老師十分好客，為遠道而來的證人準備飲水與陰涼長椅。 平日開朗的車夫認出私人拍賣印章時沉默了。 工人提議修剪荒廢小徑，方便年長證人到會堂。 留著大鬍子的開朗書記答應逐字記錄。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** No one in the room was permitted to declare that a good intention canceled a harmful action. Liora insisted on recording the error, identifying who had authorized it, and protecting those affected before assigning wider blame. Nemi drew a simple boundary around what they could do tonight. The line looked disappointingly modest, yet it was the first promise that did not require another person to surrender a right.

**繁中** 室內沒有人能宣稱善意可以抵銷傷害。莉歐拉堅持記錄錯誤、確認授權者，並在追究更大責任前保護受害者。妮米畫出今晚能做到的清楚界線。這條線看似微不足道，卻是第一個不要求別人放棄權利的承諾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: A grandmother told him to stop nagging and gave him an ounce of sweet tea. The old cafeteria became an oasis for night workers exhausted by the investigation. The clerk entered ounce in the testimony without deciding what it proved. The civic court met beside a public garden on the outskirts of the capital. In the afternoon, a member of parliament appeared with a packet of engineering drawings. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 一位祖母要他別再嘮叨，遞給他一小杯甜茶。 舊餐廳成為調查期間疲憊夜班工人的休息綠洲。 書記將「ounce」記入證詞，沒有立即判定它能證明什麼。 公民法庭位於京城郊區的公共花園旁。 下午，一名國會議員帶著工程圖出席。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** There were children behind the temple steps who could remember the taste of bread but not the people who had taught them to bake it. Liora did not mistake their silence for gratitude. Every miracle that filled a bowl and emptied a memory left a different kind of hunger behind.

**繁中** 神殿階梯後方的孩子記得麵包滋味，卻不記得教自己烤麵包的人。莉歐拉沒有把沉默誤認為感激。每場填飽碗、卻掏空記憶的奇蹟，都會留下另一種飢餓。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The nobleman's methods were ruthless but often lawful on paper: he delayed funding, demanded impossible permits, and threatened public employees with dismissal. A local sculptor offered a warehouse sketch that showed the original side entrance. The repair team preferred simplicity in emergency instructions over impressive technical language. A simultaneous release of both chambers was therefore forbidden until the second inspection. Public confidence fell into a slump when the palace refused to explain the disputed caption. Liora asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 這名貴族的方法冷酷，但在紙面上常常合法：延後經費、索取不可能取得的許可，並以解雇威脅公務員。 一名在地雕塑家提供倉庫素描，顯示原有側門。 維修團隊偏好簡單的緊急指示，而非炫目的技術詞。 因此，在第二次檢查以前，兩個艙室不得同時放能。 王宮拒絕解釋爭議圖說，公眾信心因此陷入低潮。 莉歐拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** On the way out, Liora passed people waiting for a result that would affect their meals, their homes, or their ability to say no. None of them had the luxury of treating the dispute as an intellectual puzzle. Nemi paused to answer a child's practical question before they left. The answer was incomplete, but unlike the old official notice, it admitted the uncertainty out loud.

**繁中** 離開時，莉歐拉經過等待結果的人群，這項結果將影響他們的餐食、家園或說不的能力。他們不能奢侈地把爭論當成智力謎題。妮米停下回答一名孩子實際的問題。答案並不完整，卻與舊公告不同：它坦白承認不確定之處。

### 理解測驗

1. **What unexpected event begins this chapter?**
   - A. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - B. Liora traces the bread miracles to a ledger that subtracts birthdays from families during the winter famine.
   - C. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 莉歐拉追查冬季飢荒的麵包奇蹟，發現帳簿把家庭成員的生日記憶扣走。

2. **What does the later discovery establish?**
   - A. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - B. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - C. A baker remembers kneading dough beside a daughter but can no longer recall her face.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 麵包師記得和女兒一起揉麵團，卻再也想不起女兒的臉。


## CHAPTER 07｜The God of Honest Measures｜誠實度量之神

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：847**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** Nobody had called it a crisis at first. A clerk had used a harmless word, a guard had followed an ordinary instruction, and by evening somebody else was carrying the cost. Liora refused to begin with the official conclusion. An old stone god speaks through a broken scale and insists that the gods did not authorize the miracle debts. In the twelve-bell capital, even an accurate number could become a lie when it left out the person whose life it counted.

**繁中** 起初沒有人稱這是危機。書記使用看似無害的詞，守衛照普通命令行事，到了晚上代價卻落在別人身上。莉歐拉拒絕從官方結論開始。古老的石神藉破損天秤開口，堅稱眾神從未授權奇蹟債務。在十二鐘都，精確的數字若遺漏真正承受後果的人，也可能成為謊言。

**EN** There were children behind the temple steps who could remember the taste of bread but not the people who had taught them to bake it. Liora did not mistake their silence for gratitude. Every miracle that filled a bowl and emptied a memory left a different kind of hunger behind.

**繁中** 神殿階梯後方的孩子記得麵包滋味，卻不記得教自己烤麵包的人。莉歐拉沒有把沉默誤認為感激。每場填飽碗、卻掏空記憶的奇蹟，都會留下另一種飢餓。

**EN** The evidence did not arrive as a confession. It came through a date that did not fit, a measurement that had been repeated, and a witness who knew exactly what the official account had left out. Liora asked Nemi not to describe suspicion as proof. If the accusation was wrong, an innocent person would suffer. If it was right, careless language could still give the guilty a way to escape.

**繁中** 證據不是以自白形式出現，而是一個對不上的日期、一項重複測量的結果，以及一位知道官方說法遺漏何事的證人。莉歐拉請妮米不要把懷疑說成證明。若指控錯了，無辜者會受害；若指控正確，草率措辭仍可能讓有罪者脫身。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The trust owned one acre of land beside the canal, but ownership did not erase public access. A wealthy bachelor on the board objected to being excluded from the safety committee. Employees organized a charity boxing exhibition to support families hurt by the outage. A disciplinary notice punished the worker for refusing to move an unsealed crate. An emigrant returned with a letter proving that his family had never sold the instrument. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 信託持有運河旁一英畝土地，但所有權不會抹去公共通行權。 董事會上一位富有的單身漢反對自己被排除在安全委員會外。 員工舉辦慈善拳擊表演，支援神殿沉默受影響的家庭。 一份紀律處分通知懲罰工人拒搬未封箱子。 一名移民帶信返鄉，證明家人從未出售儀器。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** A small act of honesty unsettled the room more than a shouted threat could have done. Its voice ceases as soon as Varos’s collectors enter the hall. Liora watched a witness decide not to change a difficult detail merely because it complicated the preferred story. Nemi recorded the correction openly. Their inquiry would be slower now, but its shape belonged less to the powerful and more to the people who had lived through the event.

**繁中** 一個小小的誠實舉動，帶來比喊叫威脅更強的震動。瓦洛斯的收債人走進大廳，神的聲音便立刻消失。莉歐拉看見證人沒有因為細節讓主流說法變複雜就改口。妮米公開記錄修正。調查會因此變慢，卻能較少受權貴支配，也更貼近親歷者。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: Maelin refused to equate a temporary lease with permanent ownership simply because one English verb sounded convenient. The manager's confident account began to falter when Liora produced the dated gate book. The marsh records linked seasonal navigation with the fertility of riverside fields. Iva would not forsake her community's claim merely to secure a private appointment with the governor. A gay employee asked whether the revised benefits would cover his husband equally. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 莉歐拉拒絕只因一個英語動詞方便，就把暫時租賃等同永久所有權。 瑪拉拿出附日期的大門簿後，經理自信的說法開始動搖。 沼地紀錄把季節航行與河岸田地肥沃度連在一起。 伊娃不願只為得到總督私下接見就放棄社區權利主張。 一名男同性戀員工詢問新福利是否同等保障他的丈夫。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The debate sharpened when Chancellor Varos demanded a decision before the evidence could be copied. Liora replied that urgency and secrecy were not the same thing. They established a temporary limit, published the reason for it, and scheduled an independent review. The result satisfied nobody completely. That was acceptable; the purpose of the process was to make abuse harder, not to produce applause.

**繁中** 當瓦洛斯宰相要求在證據複製前就做決定，辯論變得尖銳。莉歐拉回應說緊急和保密不是同一回事。團隊訂立暫時限制、公開理由，並排定獨立審查。結果沒有讓任何人完全滿意，但程序的目的本來就不是得到掌聲，而是使濫權更困難。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The workplace gloom lifted slightly after the tribunal protected the workers' safety veto. A survey found the dangerous intersection near the substation lacked proper lighting. A landslide had closed the direct quarry road, giving the driver one legitimate reason to detour. The quarry families grew vegetables to nourish children during the wage dispute. The orthodox court scholars disliked the river dialect but admitted its grammar was legally relevant. Liora asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 法庭保護工人安全否決權後，工作場所的陰鬱稍稍散去。 調查發現變電站旁危險路口缺少適當照明。 山崩曾封閉採石場直路，讓司機有合理繞道原因。 採石場家庭種蔬菜，在欠薪爭議期間滋養孩子。 正統宮廷學者不喜歡河語方言，卻承認其文法具有法律相關性。 莉歐拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** There were children behind the temple steps who could remember the taste of bread but not the people who had taught them to bake it. Liora did not mistake their silence for gratitude. Every miracle that filled a bowl and emptied a memory left a different kind of hunger behind.

**繁中** 神殿階梯後方的孩子記得麵包滋味，卻不記得教自己烤麵包的人。莉歐拉沒有把沉默誤認為感激。每場填飽碗、卻掏空記憶的奇蹟，都會留下另一種飢餓。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The prohibition on unsafe work could not be canceled through a private annex. Lira asked for the provisional orders issued before the border fire; Lira said they had been destroyed in a flood. The vote was unanimous, yet the council could not erase an independent court order. The governor's vanity had led him to display a larger map than anyone could read closely. A woodpecker tapped the hostel roof while the workers discussed their missing wages. Nemi marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 禁止危險作業的規定不能透過私人附約取消。 莉拉要求查看邊境大火前發布的臨時命令，莉拉卻說文件在洪水中毀了。 表決結果一致，議會卻無法抹除獨立法庭的命令。 總督的虛榮使他展示了一張大得無法近看細讀的地圖。 工人討論積欠薪資時，一隻啄木鳥敲著宿舍屋頂。 妮米標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A message arrived after the public doors had closed. It carried no grand declaration, just a name and a time that did not match the official schedule. Liora resisted the urge to call it proof. Nemi opened a fresh page in the journal and recorded the discrepancy exactly. They had won time, not certainty, and there were still people who might lose everything if they hurried.

**繁中** 公共大門關閉後，一則訊息送到。它沒有豪言壯語，只有一個名字和一個對不上官方時程的時間。莉歐拉克制住把它當成證明的衝動。妮米翻開日誌新的一頁，逐字記錄差異。他們贏得的是時間，不是確定性；若急於求成，仍有人可能失去一切。

### 理解測驗

1. **What important problem does the opening scene reveal?**
   - A. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - B. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - C. An old stone god speaks through a broken scale and insists that the gods did not authorize the miracle debts.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 古老的石神藉破損天秤開口，堅稱眾神從未授權奇蹟債務。

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. Its voice ceases as soon as Varos’s collectors enter the hall.
   - B. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   - C. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 瓦洛斯的收債人走進大廳，神的聲音便立刻消失。


## CHAPTER 08｜A Choir of Missing Years｜消失歲月的合唱

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：853**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** A messenger arrived before the bells had finished ringing. The news sounded impossible only because everyone had been trained not to question the institution that delivered it. Nemi gathers singers who recall different versions of the same hymn and links the missing verses to erased childhoods. Liora asked the messenger to repeat the part that had been omitted from the written notice. It was there, in the omission, that the day's real story began.

**繁中** 鐘聲尚未結束，信使已經抵達。消息之所以聽來不可能，只因每個人都習慣不質疑傳遞消息的制度。妮米召集記得不同版本聖歌的歌者，把缺失的歌詞和遭刪除的童年連起來。莉歐拉請信使重述書面公告遺漏的部分。這一天真正的故事，就從那處缺漏開始。

**EN** There were children behind the temple steps who could remember the taste of bread but not the people who had taught them to bake it. Liora did not mistake their silence for gratitude. Every miracle that filled a bowl and emptied a memory left a different kind of hunger behind.

**繁中** 神殿階梯後方的孩子記得麵包滋味，卻不記得教自己烤麵包的人。莉歐拉沒有把沉默誤認為感激。每場填飽碗、卻掏空記憶的奇蹟，都會留下另一種飢餓。

**EN** Beyond the meeting room, people were already finding ways to survive. They traded tools, shared the location of safe passages, and argued over fair turns without waiting for permission. Liora listened longer than the schedule allowed. Nemi reminded the officials that no policy was complete until it made sense to the people expected to live with it. Their notes began with those ordinary acts of cooperation.

**繁中** 會議室外，人們早已開始尋求生存方法：交換工具、分享安全路線的位置，無須等候許可便爭論公平輪班。莉歐拉聽得比議程允許的更久。妮米提醒官員，政策若不能讓實際生活其中的人理解，就不算完整。他們從這些平凡合作開始記錄。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: An accumulation of sediment had changed the waterways, and the official map was no longer accurate. A vendor offered each inspector a sealed beverage, but they declined gifts during the audit. The blond driver in the delivery photograph confirmed that he had used the wrong route. Lieutenant Lira sought clearance for the witnesses, while Daven asked a local surveyor to certify the paths through the marsh. The clerk entered clearance in the testimony without deciding what it proved. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 累積的泥沙改變了水道，官方地圖已不準確。 供應商給每位檢查員密封飲料，但眾人在稽核期間拒絕收禮。 送貨照片裡的金髮司機證實自己走錯了路。 莉拉中尉替證人申請通行許可，達文則請地方測量員認證穿越濕地的路線。 書記將「clearance」記入證詞，沒有立即判定它能證明什麼。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** Then the next piece of evidence changed the shape of the case. Their rehearsal unlocks a vault where the debt records are kept behind the altar. Liora read the statement twice, searching for the sentence that would make the contradiction disappear. There was none. Nemi did not reach for a dramatic accusation; instead, they listed three people who could verify the finding separately. The delay would be unpopular, but a dramatic discovery deserved more care, not less.

**繁中** 接著出現的新證據改變了整起案件的輪廓。排練的聲音竟打開祭壇後方存放債務紀錄的密室。莉歐拉把陳述讀了兩遍，試圖找出能消除矛盾的句子，卻沒有找到。妮米沒有立刻提出轟動指控，而是列出三位可分別驗證發現的人。延遲會不受歡迎，但重大發現更需要謹慎。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: A copy of those figures survived in the harbor office, where a careful official had refused to deduct unexplained losses. Maelin realized the copied warning was destined to matter more than the elegant ceremonial title. The survey team had to exert care when lifting the old storage chest from damp floorboards. The villagers called the teacher's quiet refusal to surrender the original heroic, though she asked for no praise. A retired army lieutenant volunteered to organize transport without taking control of the inquiry. Liora asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 港務處保留了一份數字副本，一名謹慎的官員拒絕扣除來源不明的損失。 莉歐拉發現副本裡的警告注定比華麗的儀式標題更重要。 測量團隊從潮濕地板抬起舊儲藏箱時必須格外用力且小心。 村民稱老師拒絕交出原件的沉默堅持很英勇，她卻不求讚美。 一名退役陸軍中尉志願安排運輸，沒有接管調查。 莉歐拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Liora had expected the hardest part to be exposing the secret. Instead, it was choosing what to do after a partial truth became public. The people who had been ignored did not all want the same remedy. Nemi proposed that each affected group could submit a written objection and receive a specific answer. The method would take time, but it recognized them as participants rather than scenery.

**繁中** 莉歐拉原以為最難的是揭露祕密，後來才發現，部分真相公開後如何行動才是難題。曾被忽略的人並不全想要相同補救。妮米提議每個受影響群體都能提出書面異議並取得具體答覆。方法需要時間，但終於把他們當成參與者，而不是背景。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: Literate workers could read the manual, but the team also prepared diagrams for everyone else. The provincial court stood beside a swamp where lotus flowers opened at dawn. A marginal note describes faith as a promise to care for others even during divine silence. The delivery times overlap, so one driver could not have handled both wagons. A provincial museum offered to store the disputed frame while the title hearing proceeded. Nemi marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 識字的工人能閱讀手冊，團隊也替其他人準備圖解。 省級法庭位於沼澤旁，黎明時蓮花盛開。 頁邊筆記將信仰描述為即使神明沉默也照顧他人的承諾。 兩趟送貨時間重疊，因此同一司機不可能同時駕兩車。 一所地方博物館願在所有權聽證期間保管爭議框架。 妮米標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** There were children behind the temple steps who could remember the taste of bread but not the people who had taught them to bake it. Liora did not mistake their silence for gratitude. Every miracle that filled a bowl and emptied a memory left a different kind of hunger behind.

**繁中** 神殿階梯後方的孩子記得麵包滋味，卻不記得教自己烤麵包的人。莉歐拉沒有把沉默誤認為感激。每場填飽碗、卻掏空記憶的奇蹟，都會留下另一種飢餓。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The new technician's qualification was checked by an independent professional board. Outside, the retired teacher offered the group simple refreshments. The team tried to retrieve a broken seal without disturbing the surrounding evidence. The original owner had learned navigation in the swamp beyond the museum's mapped territory. The children learned that a translator's vocation involved responsibility to readers, not blind loyalty to rulers. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 新技師的資格由獨立專業委員會核對。 法庭外，退休教師為一行人準備簡單茶點。 團隊試圖取回破損封條，同時不干擾周圍證物。 原所有者曾在博物館地圖之外的沼澤學習導航。 孩子們學到翻譯家的職志是對讀者負責，而非盲目效忠統治者。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** When the lamps were lowered, Liora returned to a cracked voting bell. It was neither proof of innocence nor a shortcut to justice. It was a reminder that the next decision would be witnessed by people who no longer trusted easy promises. Nemi remained at the door while the final record was sealed. Outside, the twelve-bell capital waited for news that had to be more precise than a victory speech.

**繁中** 燈光調暗時，莉歐拉再次看向一口裂開的投票鐘。它既不是清白證明，也不是通往正義的捷徑，而是在提醒眾人：下一個決定會由不再相信輕易承諾的人見證。妮米守在門邊，最後一份紀錄被封存。外頭的十二鐘都等待的消息，必須比勝利演說更精確。

   - B. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - C. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.

## CHAPTER 09｜The Miracle Engine｜奇蹟引擎

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：840**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The first sign of trouble was not an explosion or a scream. It was a familiar procedure producing a result nobody could defend. Liora enters the underground engine that converts unpaid memories into public displays of divine power. Liora checked the time, the witnesses, and the rule that authorized the action. The three did not agree. Outside, ordinary life continued, which made the error more frightening rather than less.

**繁中** 麻煩的第一個徵兆不是爆炸或尖叫，而是熟悉程序得出了誰也無法辯護的結果。莉歐拉進入地下引擎室，發現有人把未付清的記憶轉成公開展示的神力。莉歐拉核對時間、證人與授權規則，三者卻互相矛盾。外頭的平凡生活照常進行，反而讓這個錯誤更加可怕。

**EN** There were children behind the temple steps who could remember the taste of bread but not the people who had taught them to bake it. Liora did not mistake their silence for gratitude. Every miracle that filled a bowl and emptied a memory left a different kind of hunger behind.

**繁中** 神殿階梯後方的孩子記得麵包滋味，卻不記得教自己烤麵包的人。莉歐拉沒有把沉默誤認為感激。每場填飽碗、卻掏空記憶的奇蹟，都會留下另一種飢餓。

**EN** Nemi arrived with a second account that neither confirmed nor dismissed the first. They compared the routes by which each record had reached the room, then placed the uncertainties beside the facts. Liora could feel the pressure to announce a solution before anyone became frightened. That pressure was precisely why a second witness mattered: a conclusion that could not survive an independent question would be dangerous, even if it happened to be right.

**繁中** 妮米帶來第二份說法，既未證實也未推翻第一份。兩人比對紀錄抵達房間的途徑，再把不確定之處和事實並列。莉歐拉感受到眾人希望立即宣布解方，以免恐懼擴大的壓力。但也正因這種壓力，第二位證人才重要：無法通過獨立質問的結論，即使碰巧正確，也可能很危險。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The public statement might alienate frightened investors, but hiding the altered seal would be worse. A black blot on the purchase order concealed the first digit of a serial number. Residents shared chili soup while waiting for the procurement hearing to begin. The envoy explained that continuity of village access was guaranteed in the oldest agreement. The cumulative delays had increased costs far beyond the original repair estimate. Liora asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 公開聲明可能疏遠害怕的投資人，但隱瞞改動封條更糟。 採購單上的黑墨漬蓋住序號第一個數字。 居民等候採購聽證開始時，分享辣椒湯。 使者解釋，最早的協議保障村民持續使用這條路；後來的修正案逐漸刪除渡口相關文字，累積的省略使權利在沒有任何正式廢止令的情況下消失。 累積延誤讓成本遠超原始維修估算。 莉歐拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** The contradiction reached the table just as the meeting was about to end. She learns that the twelve gods disconnected the machine rather than abandon humanity. For a long moment no one spoke. Liora understood why silence felt comfortable; each answer threatened someone's settled account of how the world worked. Nemi broke the stillness by asking who would be harmed if they simply filed the discovery away. No one could answer without naming a real person.

**繁中** 會議即將結束時，矛盾證據送上桌面。她得知十二神並非拋棄人類，而是主動切斷機器連線。一時之間無人開口。莉歐拉明白沉默為何讓人舒服：任何答案都可能動搖某人對世界的既定理解。妮米打破沉默，問若把發現歸檔，究竟誰會受害。沒人能在不指名真實人物的情況下回答。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: Lira made a decisive choice to publish the damaged-cabinet test before the vote. No worker was dispensable merely because an executive feared an embarrassing question. A visitor wore jasmine perfume that matched the scent noted by the night guard. The river lodge provided temporary rooms for families displaced during repairs. A maple tree near the entrance helped the courier identify the delivery location. Nemi marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 莉拉果斷決定在表決前公布受損櫃體測試。 不能因主管害怕尷尬提問，就把任何工人當成可有可無。 某位訪客使用茉莉香水，與夜班警衛記下的氣味一致。 河邊旅舍為維修期間搬離的家庭提供臨時房間。 入口旁的楓樹協助快遞員辨認送貨地點。 妮米標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** There were two tempting shortcuts. One would protect the reputation of Chancellor Varos and leave the public guessing. The other would expose every private detail at once and call that transparency. Liora rejected both. The issue was whether a miracle may be taken from a citizen without consent, not whether the team could win an argument in a single afternoon. A smaller step, supported by independent observers, could protect people while the wider dispute remained open.

**繁中** 有兩種誘人的捷徑：一種保住瓦洛斯宰相的名聲，讓大眾自行猜測；另一種把所有私人細節一次揭露，並稱之為透明。莉歐拉兩者都拒絕。核心是奇蹟能否未經同意就向公民索取代價，不是團隊能否在一個下午贏得爭論。由獨立觀察者支持的一小步，即使爭議未結，也能先保護民眾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The next witness was Lira, an envoy from the eastern federation whom the Empire officially called a rebel mistress of spies. In the next room, people debated how to mobilize villagers for a public meeting without provoking soldiers. Lira paused to muse over how often public trust had been treated as private property. The whistleblower's persistence finally persuaded investigators to examine the old account. A socialist council member supported public oversight without claiming every private firm was guilty. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 下一名證人是東方聯邦使者伊絲拉；帝國官方稱她是指揮間諜的叛軍女頭目。 隔壁房裡，人們討論如何動員村民出席公開集會，又不激怒士兵。 莉拉停下來沉思，公共信任究竟有多少次被當成私人財產。 吹哨者的堅持終於讓調查員檢查舊帳戶。 一名社會主義議員支持公共監督，沒有宣稱所有私人企業都有罪。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** There were children behind the temple steps who could remember the taste of bread but not the people who had taught them to bake it. Liora did not mistake their silence for gratitude. Every miracle that filled a bowl and emptied a memory left a different kind of hunger behind.

**繁中** 神殿階梯後方的孩子記得麵包滋味，卻不記得教自己烤麵包的人。莉歐拉沒有把沉默誤認為感激。每場填飽碗、卻掏空記憶的奇蹟，都會留下另一種飢餓。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: Employees had a chance to socialize after the hearing without being questioned by reporters. The access log covered a span of seven hours, with one unexplained gap near midnight. The author had been ordered to trek into the mountains and test the prophecy relay before civilians were evacuated. Their disagreement was vigorous but not violent. A waterproof cover protected the pages, and the calculations were still legible. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 聽證後，員工終於能不受記者盤問地社交。 門禁紀錄涵蓋七小時，其中午夜附近有一段無法解釋的空白。 作者奉命徒步進入山區，在平民撤離前測試預言中繼站。 他們爭論得激烈，卻沒有暴力。 防水封套保存了紙頁，計算仍清晰可讀。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The chapter ended without an easy reassurance. Liora had learned one thing that could be checked and another that still needed an answer. Nemi wrote both on the same page so that relief would not erase doubt. Their decision had moved the crisis forward, but it had also drawn the attention of Chancellor Varos. By morning, the next question would reach people far beyond this room.

**繁中** 這一天沒有簡單的安慰。莉歐拉知道了一件能查證的事，也留下另一件尚待回答的事。妮米把兩者寫在同一頁，免得鬆一口氣就抹掉疑問。他們的決定使危機向前，也引起瓦洛斯宰相注意。明早下一個問題將傳到房間外更遠的地方。

### 理解測驗

1. **What unexpected event begins this chapter?**
   - A. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - B. Liora enters the underground engine that converts unpaid memories into public displays of divine power.
   - C. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 莉歐拉進入地下引擎室，發現有人把未付清的記憶轉成公開展示的神力。

2. **What does the later discovery establish?**
   - A. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - B. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   - C. She learns that the twelve gods disconnected the machine rather than abandon humanity.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 她得知十二神並非拋棄人類，而是主動切斷機器連線。


## CHAPTER 10｜The Candidate Is the Debt｜候選人就是債務

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：854**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** Morning brought a decision that had already been made for everyone. There was a signature at the bottom of the order, but no space for those most affected to answer. At the first televised debate, Liora proves that every new god must inherit the entire hidden debt of the previous system. Liora knew that refusing a bad choice was not the same as knowing the correct one. Before making a promise, the team needed something they could test.

**繁中** 清晨帶來一項替所有人做好的決定。命令底下有簽名，卻沒有讓受影響最深者回答的地方。在第一場公開辯論中，莉歐拉證明每位新神都必須繼承舊制度全部祕密債務。莉歐拉知道拒絕壞選項不等於已經知道正確答案。作出承諾前，團隊需要能夠驗證的事物。

**EN** On the public debating floor, Liora placed the miracle invoices beneath the voting bell and asked the candidates to name the cost of the office they hoped to win. Nobody answered. She unfolded the founding charter, which described divine office as a debt inherited by whoever took it. The chancellor seized the paper and declared that the only person allowed to expose that debt was the person required to pay it. Then he read the final candidate list. Liora's name stood alone. She had never registered; the system had registered her by taking her question as consent. A thousand voters saw it happen. Nemi leaned close and said, 'The ballot isn't choosing a god. It's choosing the next person the city can sacrifice.'

**繁中** 在公開辯論場，莉歐拉把奇蹟帳單放在投票鐘下，請候選人說明自己想爭取的職位要付出什麼代價。沒有人回答。她展開建國憲章，發現神職被寫成由繼任者承接的債務。宰相奪走紙張，宣稱只有必須償還債務的人才有資格揭露。接著他讀出最終候選名單，裡頭只有莉歐拉。她從未報名，系統卻把她的提問視為同意。上千名選民親眼看見。妮米靠近她耳邊低聲說：「這張選票不是在選神，是在選下一個可以犧牲的人。」

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The supplier sought a concession that would grant it control over the canal for a decade. A cynical investor said patients were useful only when their stories moved the market. The old procurement rule treated silence as consent by default, which the new board rejected. An emergency fund should not deplete the clinic budget merely to improve quarterly profit. Soldiers handed out sweet buns, and the fragrance of crushed flowers covered the smell of oil along the river. Nemi marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 供應商尋求特許權，想控制運河十年。 一位憤世嫉俗的投資人說，病人故事只有能推動市場時才有用。 舊採購規則預設沉默即同意，新董事會予以否決。 緊急基金不該只為改善季度利潤就耗盡診所預算。 士兵發放甜麵包，壓碎花朵的香氣蓋住河邊油味。 妮米標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** What changed the discussion was a fact that should have been obvious from the start. Varos announces her name as the only eligible candidate, though she never registered. Liora felt the old temptation to turn that fact into an immediate victory. Instead, the team separated what the new evidence established from what it merely suggested. That distinction frustrated the loudest spectators, but it helped the witnesses remain willing to speak again.

**繁中** 改變討論的是一件本來就該看得見的事實。瓦洛斯竟宣布莉歐拉是唯一合格候選人，儘管她從未報名。莉歐拉一瞬間也想把它當成立刻勝利的證明，團隊卻把新證據真正確立的部分與僅能推測的部分分開。這讓最喧鬧的旁觀者失望，卻使證人願意再次出面。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: No official would be permitted to intrude on protected witness meetings without a court order. A former jockey now driving delivery vans recognized the unusual generator model. The clerk recorded the literal wording of the ownership claim without adding her opinion. The chancellor's lawyer made a sharp retort about national morale, claiming that questioning the prophecy relay would deplete public confidence. The court could prosecute fraud only after evidence met the required standard. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 沒有法院命令，任何官員都不得闖入受保護的證人會面。 一名曾當賽馬騎師的廂車司機認出了不尋常的發電機型號。 書記逐字記下所有權主張，沒有加入個人意見。 宰相律師尖銳反駁，談起國民士氣，聲稱質疑預言中繼站會耗盡公眾信心。 證據達到法定標準後，法庭才能起訴詐欺。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** No one in the room was permitted to declare that a good intention canceled a harmful action. Liora insisted on recording the error, identifying who had authorized it, and protecting those affected before assigning wider blame. Nemi drew a simple boundary around what they could do tonight. The line looked disappointingly modest, yet it was the first promise that did not require another person to surrender a right.

**繁中** 室內沒有人能宣稱善意可以抵銷傷害。莉歐拉堅持記錄錯誤、確認授權者，並在追究更大責任前保護受害者。妮米畫出今晚能做到的清楚界線。這條線看似微不足道，卻是第一個不要求別人放棄權利的承諾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The maintenance inspector checked for radioactive materials because an old sensor carried a misleading label. The chancellor's lawyer made a sharp retort about national morale, claiming that questioning the Sun Engine would deplete public confidence. A young courier came forward with a screwdriver and a box of broken clock parts. Outside, an elderly vendor offered each departing witness a serving of warm soup. The tower's splendor seemed irrelevant beside the dark apartments across the river. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 維修檢查員因舊感測器標示誤導而檢查有無放射性物質。 宰相律師尖銳反駁，談起國民士氣，聲稱質疑太陽引擎會耗盡公眾信心。 一名年輕信差帶著螺絲起子和一箱壞時鐘零件上前。 庭外，一名年長攤販給離開的每位證人一份熱湯。 相較河對岸漆黑的公寓，大樓的華麗顯得毫無意義。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** There were children behind the temple steps who could remember the taste of bread but not the people who had taught them to bake it. Liora did not mistake their silence for gratitude. Every miracle that filled a bowl and emptied a memory left a different kind of hunger behind.

**繁中** 神殿階梯後方的孩子記得麵包滋味，卻不記得教自己烤麵包的人。莉歐拉沒有把沉默誤認為感激。每場填飽碗、卻掏空記憶的奇蹟，都會留下另一種飢餓。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: A spontaneous round of applause followed the announcement that workers could read the records. The boy began to stutter when he saw his stepfather at the back of the hall. The nervous clerk began to stutter before the judge invited her to speak slowly. The city agreed to subsidize emergency batteries for low-income patients during the trial. The military had claimed its newest heat reservoirs contained harmless synthetic stone. Liora asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 宣布工人可以查閱紀錄後，現場自發響起掌聲。 少年在庭後看見繼父，說話開始結巴。 緊張的書記開始口吃，法官請她慢慢說。 市府同意在試辦期間補助低收入病人的緊急電池。 軍方聲稱最新儲能庫採用無害的合成石材；學院在丟棄的隔熱材料中發現少量放射性礦物痕跡，但含量不足以確認疾病原因。 莉歐拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** On the way out, Liora passed people waiting for a result that would affect their meals, their homes, or their ability to say no. None of them had the luxury of treating the dispute as an intellectual puzzle. Nemi paused to answer a child's practical question before they left. The answer was incomplete, but unlike the old official notice, it admitted the uncertainty out loud.

**繁中** 離開時，莉歐拉經過等待結果的人群，這項結果將影響他們的餐食、家園或說不的能力。他們不能奢侈地把爭論當成智力謎題。妮米停下回答一名孩子實際的問題。答案並不完整，卻與舊公告不同：它坦白承認不確定之處。
