### 理解測驗

1. **Why does the first event matter?**
   - A. Varos tries to restart the engine with his own memories, believing sacrifice will justify command.
   - B. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - C. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 瓦洛斯試圖拿自己的記憶重啟引擎，以為個人犧牲就能合理化統治。

2. **Which finding changes the characters’ understanding?**
   - A. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - B. The engine refuses because his memories cannot replace consent from the people he exploited.
   - C. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 引擎拒絕啟動，因為他的記憶不能取代受害者的同意。


## CHAPTER 21｜The Election Without a God｜沒有神的選舉

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：845**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The first sign of trouble was not an explosion or a scream. It was a familiar procedure producing a result nobody could defend. The first honest ballot offers restoration, shared stewardship, and no divine ruler as equal choices. Liora checked the time, the witnesses, and the rule that authorized the action. The three did not agree. Outside, ordinary life continued, which made the error more frightening rather than less.

**繁中** 麻煩的第一個徵兆不是爆炸或尖叫，而是熟悉程序得出了誰也無法辯護的結果。第一次誠實選舉把恢復舊制、共同監督及不設神權列為平等選項。莉歐拉核對時間、證人與授權規則，三者卻互相矛盾。外頭的平凡生活照常進行，反而讓這個錯誤更加可怕。

**EN** There were children behind the temple steps who could remember the taste of bread but not the people who had taught them to bake it. Liora did not mistake their silence for gratitude. Every miracle that filled a bowl and emptied a memory left a different kind of hunger behind.

**繁中** 神殿階梯後方的孩子記得麵包滋味，卻不記得教自己烤麵包的人。莉歐拉沒有把沉默誤認為感激。每場填飽碗、卻掏空記憶的奇蹟，都會留下另一種飢餓。

**EN** A witness asked whether the inquiry would make tomorrow safer. Liora could not promise that. The truth could establish responsibility, but it would not repair a broken machine, feed a hungry child, or return lost time on its own. Nemi suggested a temporary protection that could be checked after sunrise. They wrote down its limits in plain language and invited the people affected to challenge them.

**繁中** 一位證人問調查是否能讓明天更安全，莉歐拉無法保證。真相能確立責任，卻不會自行修好機器、餵飽孩子或歸還失去的時間。妮米提出一項日出後可以檢驗的暫時保護措施。他們用白話寫清限制，邀請受影響的人提出異議。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: An aviation expert confirmed that the alleged emergency flight had never occurred. Beforehand, investigators had tested every copy of the island packet against original seals and accounting registers. A public booklet explained the difference between administrative negligence and criminal intent. The commission next heard from technicians who explained the cellular structure of the storage crystals. The duration of the deliberate outage had been calculated to trigger the trust clause. Liora asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 航空專家證實所謂的緊急航班根本沒有發生。 調查員事前已將島上文件包的每份副本與原始印璽、帳簿逐一核對。 一本公開小冊子解釋行政疏忽與刑事故意的差異。 委員會接著聽技師解釋儲能晶體的細胞狀結構。 蓄意神殿沉默的持續時間經過計算，目的是觸發信託條款。 莉歐拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** For once the numbers did not merely decorate a report; they contradicted the order that had been issued in their name. The vote selects shared stewardship with strict limits, surprising both priests and revolutionaries. Nemi asked whether a mistake could explain the result. Liora agreed to test that possibility too. They checked an independent source, marked every assumption, and made copies before the originals could be transferred to an office beyond public reach.

**繁中** 這一次數字不只是報告裝飾，而是直接反駁以數字名義發布的命令。人民選擇受嚴格限制的共同監督制，讓祭司與革命者同樣驚訝。妮米詢問是否可能只是出錯，莉歐拉也同意檢查這種可能。他們核對獨立來源、標記每項假設，在原件被移往公眾無法接觸的辦公室前留下副本。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: A family folklore story about the founder could not replace the company's accounting records. No witness should have to endure harassment merely for appearing before the commission. There were villages marked in pencil where the imperial survey showed only blank wilderness. Lira entered the hearing through an imposing doorway surrounded by cameras. The court could oblige the trust to preserve records but not to confess without evidence. Nemi marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 家族關於創辦人的民間故事不能取代公司帳目。 任何證人都不應只因出席委員會，就必須承受騷擾。 帝國測量圖上空白的荒野，在這張圖上卻用鉛筆標著村莊。 莉拉穿過氣勢逼人的大門，在攝影機包圍下進入聽證。 法院可要求信託保存紀錄，不能在缺乏證據下強迫認罪。 妮米標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** There were two tempting shortcuts. One would protect the reputation of Chancellor Varos and leave the public guessing. The other would expose every private detail at once and call that transparency. Liora rejected both. The issue was whether a miracle may be taken from a citizen without consent, not whether the team could win an argument in a single afternoon. A smaller step, supported by independent observers, could protect people while the wider dispute remained open.

**繁中** 有兩種誘人的捷徑：一種保住瓦洛斯宰相的名聲，讓大眾自行猜測；另一種把所有私人細節一次揭露，並稱之為透明。莉歐拉兩者都拒絕。核心是奇蹟能否未經同意就向公民索取代價，不是團隊能否在一個下午贏得爭論。由獨立觀察者支持的一小步，即使爭議未結，也能先保護民眾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: Vask seemed to obsess over the appearance of the seals rather than the actual changes in the words. A science exhibit featuring an octopus entertained children waiting for the hearing to finish. Workers described how the ministry had used threats to oppress those who reported failures. The union organizer reminded witnesses that their private addresses would remain protected. Their report also identified a peril in continuing to operate the network without new safety rules. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 瓦斯克似乎過度執著於印章外觀，而非文字真正改動。 展出章魚的科學展示讓等候聽證結束的孩子有事可做。 工人描述部門如何威脅回報故障的人，壓迫他們保持沉默。 工會組織者提醒證人，私人地址會受到保護。 報告也指出，若不訂新安全規則就繼續使用網路，將有重大危險。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** There were children behind the temple steps who could remember the taste of bread but not the people who had taught them to bake it. Liora did not mistake their silence for gratitude. Every miracle that filled a bowl and emptied a memory left a different kind of hunger behind.

**繁中** 神殿階梯後方的孩子記得麵包滋味，卻不記得教自己烤麵包的人。莉歐拉沒有把沉默誤認為感激。每場填飽碗、卻掏空記憶的奇蹟，都會留下另一種飢餓。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: A poultry farmer told the court that his family had lost livestock when their barn froze overnight. A prehistoric fossil in the courthouse display distracted a nervous child from the proceedings. Folklore could teach courage, but a proverb or story should never replace the documents needed to judge a living person's guilt. A model spacecraft stood in the lobby beside the city's new energy-efficiency exhibit. A wrinkle in the treasurer’s explanation emerged when the dates of two payment orders were compared. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 一名養禽農民告訴法庭，家裡的牲畜因穀倉整夜結凍而死。 法院展櫃裡的史前化石讓緊張孩子暫時轉移注意。 民間傳說能教人勇氣，但諺語和故事不能取代判斷活人罪責所需的文件。 大廳裡的太空船模型與城市新能源效率展示並列。 比較兩份付款命令日期時，司庫的說法出現一處破綻。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The chapter ended without an easy reassurance. Liora had learned one thing that could be checked and another that still needed an answer. Nemi wrote both on the same page so that relief would not erase doubt. Their decision had moved the crisis forward, but it had also drawn the attention of Chancellor Varos. By morning, the next question would reach people far beyond this room.

**繁中** 這一天沒有簡單的安慰。莉歐拉知道了一件能查證的事，也留下另一件尚待回答的事。妮米把兩者寫在同一頁，免得鬆一口氣就抹掉疑問。他們的決定使危機向前，也引起瓦洛斯宰相注意。明早下一個問題將傳到房間外更遠的地方。

### 理解測驗

1. **What unexpected event begins this chapter?**
   - A. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - B. The first honest ballot offers restoration, shared stewardship, and no divine ruler as equal choices.
   - C. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 第一次誠實選舉把恢復舊制、共同監督及不設神權列為平等選項。

2. **What does the later discovery establish?**
   - A. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - B. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   - C. The vote selects shared stewardship with strict limits, surprising both priests and revolutionaries.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 人民選擇受嚴格限制的共同監督制，讓祭司與革命者同樣驚訝。


## CHAPTER 22｜The Twelve Witnesses｜十二位證人

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：838**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** Morning brought a decision that had already been made for everyone. There was a signature at the bottom of the order, but no space for those most affected to answer. The gods testify about their complicity and submit the hidden miracle engine to public control. Liora knew that refusing a bad choice was not the same as knowing the correct one. Before making a promise, the team needed something they could test.

**繁中** 清晨帶來一項替所有人做好的決定。命令底下有簽名，卻沒有讓受影響最深者回答的地方。十二神說明各自的過失，把祕密奇蹟引擎交給公共監督。莉歐拉知道拒絕壞選項不等於已經知道正確答案。作出承諾前，團隊需要能夠驗證的事物。

**EN** There were children behind the temple steps who could remember the taste of bread but not the people who had taught them to bake it. Liora did not mistake their silence for gratitude. Every miracle that filled a bowl and emptied a memory left a different kind of hunger behind.

**繁中** 神殿階梯後方的孩子記得麵包滋味，卻不記得教自己烤麵包的人。莉歐拉沒有把沉默誤認為感激。每場填飽碗、卻掏空記憶的奇蹟，都會留下另一種飢餓。

**EN** The evidence did not arrive as a confession. It came through a date that did not fit, a measurement that had been repeated, and a witness who knew exactly what the official account had left out. Liora asked Nemi not to describe suspicion as proof. If the accusation was wrong, an innocent person would suffer. If it was right, careless language could still give the guilty a way to escape.

**繁中** 證據不是以自白形式出現，而是一個對不上的日期、一項重複測量的結果，以及一位知道官方說法遺漏何事的證人。莉歐拉請妮米不要把懷疑說成證明。若指控錯了，無辜者會受害；若指控正確，草率措辭仍可能讓有罪者脫身。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: A choir rehearsed a new anthem about shared rivers rather than imperial conquest. Children would be able to ascend the hillside openly, while their parents continued to reside in their own villages. An independent financial audit revealed how wartime warehouses had received excessive funding for fuel and medicine. A retired captain who had once called eastern people barbarian offered an awkward apology. The manufacturer admitted a defect in one relay but denied causing the earlier sabotage. Nemi marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 合唱團排練新的國歌，歌詞讚頌共同河流而非帝國征服。 孩子們將能公開登上山坡，父母也得以繼續住在自己的村莊。 獨立財務稽核揭露戰時倉庫如何為燃料與藥品取得過多經費。 一名退休上尉過去稱東方人為野蠻人，如今笨拙道歉。 製造商承認一個繼電器有缺陷，否認造成先前破壞。 妮米標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A small act of honesty unsettled the room more than a shouted threat could have done. Not all are forgiven, but none can again charge a child for a stranger’s prayer. Liora watched a witness decide not to change a difficult detail merely because it complicated the preferred story. Nemi recorded the correction openly. Their inquiry would be slower now, but its shape belonged less to the powerful and more to the people who had lived through the event.

**繁中** 一個小小的誠實舉動，帶來比喊叫威脅更強的震動。並非每位神都獲得原諒，但再也沒有神可以拿孩子替陌生人的祈禱付費。莉歐拉看見證人沒有因為細節讓主流說法變複雜就改口。妮米公開記錄修正。調查會因此變慢，卻能較少受權貴支配，也更貼近親歷者。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: Shareholders began to grumble when the safety reforms reduced their expected dividends. Unexpected hail struck the temporary roof while the power crew inspected the clinic. Other crates held healthful food that had spoiled while families waited. A hedge of roses bordered the path where the two discussed their future work. The pharmacist confirmed that heroin was not part of the clinic's authorized medicine inventory. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 安全改革降低預期股利時，股東開始抱怨。 供電班組檢查診所時，突如其來的冰雹打在臨時屋頂上。 另一些箱子裝著有益健康的食物，卻在家庭等候時腐壞。 兩人討論未來工作的小徑旁，有一道玫瑰樹籬。 藥師證實海洛因不在診所核准藥品庫存中。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** No one in the room was permitted to declare that a good intention canceled a harmful action. Liora insisted on recording the error, identifying who had authorized it, and protecting those affected before assigning wider blame. Nemi drew a simple boundary around what they could do tonight. The line looked disappointingly modest, yet it was the first promise that did not require another person to surrender a right.

**繁中** 室內沒有人能宣稱善意可以抵銷傷害。莉歐拉堅持記錄錯誤、確認授權者，並在追究更大責任前保護受害者。妮米畫出今晚能做到的清楚界線。這條線看似微不足道，卻是第一個不要求別人放棄權利的承諾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The court respected intimacy and barred questions about a witness's private relationship unless legally relevant. The mainland supplier agreed to compete under the same public procurement rules. Residents could mingle with engineers after the hearing without a private invitation. The clerk entered pharmacist in the testimony without deciding what it proved. The team tested a backup system designed for polar research stations under extreme cold. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 法庭尊重親密隱私，禁止詢問與案件無關的證人私人關係。 大陸供應商同意依相同公共採購規則競爭。 聽證後居民不用私人邀請就能與工程師交流。 書記將「pharmacist」記入證詞，沒有立即判定它能證明什麼。 團隊測試一套供極地研究站使用、能耐極寒的備電系統。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** There were children behind the temple steps who could remember the taste of bread but not the people who had taught them to bake it. Liora did not mistake their silence for gratitude. Every miracle that filled a bowl and emptied a memory left a different kind of hunger behind.

**繁中** 神殿階梯後方的孩子記得麵包滋味，卻不記得教自己烤麵包的人。莉歐拉沒有把沉默誤認為感激。每場填飽碗、卻掏空記憶的奇蹟，都會留下另一種飢餓。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The old interpreter had chosen to reside near the ferry so he could hear everyday river speech. The agreement provided for regular reviews and a public complaint process resistant to political interference. A boy with a limp called to his stepmother to see the delegates arrive. The economist warned that a trillion imaginary crowns on a chart would not repair the real roof. A government did not become upright merely because it announced better intentions. Liora asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 老譯員選擇住在渡口附近，才能聽到日常河語。 協議訂有定期複查與不受政治干預的公開申訴程序。 一個跛腳男孩喊繼母來看代表抵達。 經濟學家警告，圖表上虛構的一兆枚王幣修不好真正的屋頂。 莉拉要求公開更正，並分別調查涉案者。 莉歐拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** On the way out, Liora passed people waiting for a result that would affect their meals, their homes, or their ability to say no. None of them had the luxury of treating the dispute as an intellectual puzzle. Nemi paused to answer a child's practical question before they left. The answer was incomplete, but unlike the old official notice, it admitted the uncertainty out loud.

**繁中** 離開時，莉歐拉經過等待結果的人群，這項結果將影響他們的餐食、家園或說不的能力。他們不能奢侈地把爭論當成智力謎題。妮米停下回答一名孩子實際的問題。答案並不完整，卻與舊公告不同：它坦白承認不確定之處。

### 理解測驗

1. **What important problem does the opening scene reveal?**
   - A. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - B. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - C. The gods testify about their complicity and submit the hidden miracle engine to public control.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 十二神說明各自的過失，把祕密奇蹟引擎交給公共監督。

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. Not all are forgiven, but none can again charge a child for a stranger’s prayer.
   - B. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - C. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 並非每位神都獲得原諒，但再也沒有神可以拿孩子替陌生人的祈禱付費。


## CHAPTER 23｜The Ledger of Restitution｜補償帳簿

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：841**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** By noon, rumors had outrun the facts. Some people wanted a villain; others wanted a hero who could end the argument without asking another question. Liora opens a restitution office where people choose what memories they wish to recover or leave untouched. Liora had worked long enough to distrust both shortcuts. In the twelve-bell capital, the first task was to protect the evidence before anyone could turn it into a weapon.

**繁中** 到了中午，謠言早已跑得比事實快。有人想找反派，也有人想找英雄，好在不再追問的情況下結束爭論。莉歐拉設立補償辦公室，由當事人選擇想恢復或不願觸碰的記憶。莉歐拉工作多年，已經不相信這兩種捷徑。在十二鐘都，當務之急是先保護證據，免得任何人將它變成武器。

**EN** There were children behind the temple steps who could remember the taste of bread but not the people who had taught them to bake it. Liora did not mistake their silence for gratitude. Every miracle that filled a bowl and emptied a memory left a different kind of hunger behind.

**繁中** 神殿階梯後方的孩子記得麵包滋味，卻不記得教自己烤麵包的人。莉歐拉沒有把沉默誤認為感激。每場填飽碗、卻掏空記憶的奇蹟，都會留下另一種飢餓。

**EN** Beyond the meeting room, people were already finding ways to survive. They traded tools, shared the location of safe passages, and argued over fair turns without waiting for permission. Liora listened longer than the schedule allowed. Nemi reminded the officials that no policy was complete until it made sense to the people expected to live with it. Their notes began with those ordinary acts of cooperation.

**繁中** 會議室外，人們早已開始尋求生存方法：交換工具、分享安全路線的位置，無須等候許可便爭論公平輪班。莉歐拉聽得比議程允許的更久。妮米提醒官員，政策若不能讓實際生活其中的人理解，就不算完整。他們從這些平凡合作開始記錄。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: To avert another wave of injustice, a commission of local representatives began reviewing claims in a public hall. A longtime caretaker testified that the southern cabinet had been unlocked after midnight. Beneath a collapsed ceiling, inspectors found a cavity in the wall containing old ration tickets and labor lists. The legal clerk had to condense the technical report without removing disputed findings. Workers expressed dismay when they learned how long the unsafe bypass rule had remained active. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 為避免另一波不義，地方代表組成委員會，在公共大廳審查申請。 長年照護員作證，南側展櫃曾在午夜後被打開。 倒塌屋頂下，檢查員發現牆裡有空腔，藏著舊配給券與工人名單。 法務書記必須濃縮技術報告，不能刪掉有爭議的發現。 工人得知不安全繞過規則長期有效時感到失望。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** Then the next piece of evidence changed the shape of the case. Nemi refuses to sing the old national hymn until the missing verses have names. Liora read the statement twice, searching for the sentence that would make the contradiction disappear. There was none. Nemi did not reach for a dramatic accusation; instead, they listed three people who could verify the finding separately. The delay would be unpopular, but a dramatic discovery deserved more care, not less.

**繁中** 接著出現的新證據改變了整起案件的輪廓。妮米堅持在缺失歌詞找到原主之前，不再演唱舊國歌。莉歐拉把陳述讀了兩遍，試圖找出能消除矛盾的句子，卻沒有找到。妮米沒有立刻提出轟動指控，而是列出三位可分別驗證發現的人。延遲會不受歡迎，但重大發現更需要謹慎。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: No one was forced into emigration to receive help, and no lottery determined who deserved a roof. The examiner identified a flaw in the original decision process, even without criminal intent. Halda requested an inclusive meeting where young navigators and elders could both vote. Some former officers still tried to intimidate applicants, and the commission referred those threats for investigation. The officials would be liable for correcting records, not for protecting the prestige of her family. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 沒有人必須移民才能得到幫助，也沒有抽籤決定誰值得有屋頂。 即使沒有刑事故意，審查員仍找出原決策流程的缺陷。 哈爾妲要求包容性的會議，讓年輕引航者和長者都能投票。 部分前軍官仍企圖恐嚇申請者，委員會將這些威脅交付調查。 官員要負責更正紀錄，而不是保護王室聲望。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The debate sharpened when Chancellor Varos demanded a decision before the evidence could be copied. Liora replied that urgency and secrecy were not the same thing. They established a temporary limit, published the reason for it, and scheduled an independent review. The result satisfied nobody completely. That was acceptable; the purpose of the process was to make abuse harder, not to produce applause.

**繁中** 當瓦洛斯宰相要求在證據複製前就做決定，辯論變得尖銳。莉歐拉回應說緊急和保密不是同一回事。團隊訂立暫時限制、公開理由，並排定獨立審查。結果沒有讓任何人完全滿意，但程序的目的本來就不是得到掌聲，而是使濫權更困難。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The clerk entered lottery in the testimony without deciding what it proved. A nearsighted clerk read each form carefully, checking for gaps rather than rushing. A child asked the clinic nurse about a pimple while her mother filed a power-loss claim. The revelation of the original loan changed the debate from possession to responsibility. An official had once ordered workers to shred duplicate records; several had refused and preserved them instead. Liora asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 書記將「lottery」記入證詞，沒有立即判定它能證明什麼。 一名近視書記仔細讀每份表格，找出缺口而不催促。 孩子問診所護士臉上的痘痘，母親則填寫神殿沉默損失申請。 原始借用關係被揭露後，爭論焦點從占有轉向責任。 過去一名官員曾命工人碎毀副本紀錄；有幾人拒絕，反而把它們保存下來。 莉歐拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** There were children behind the temple steps who could remember the taste of bread but not the people who had taught them to bake it. Liora did not mistake their silence for gratitude. Every miracle that filled a bowl and emptied a memory left a different kind of hunger behind.

**繁中** 神殿階梯後方的孩子記得麵包滋味，卻不記得教自己烤麵包的人。莉歐拉沒有把沉默誤認為感激。每場填飽碗、卻掏空記憶的奇蹟，都會留下另一種飢餓。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The solemn ruling gave his freedom a legal foundation that private promises had never offered. Blocking the road again would suffocate the town’s recovering trade, the local council warned. The repair fund faced a surge of applications after the compensation announcement. The engineers measured an upward movement in the gate during the final safety check. The rebuilding workforce included former soldiers and refugees, all paid under the same published rules. Nemi marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 這項莊嚴判決為他的自由建立法律基礎，是私人承諾從未提供過的保障。 地方議會警告，再次封路會扼殺小鎮正在復甦的貿易。 賠償公告後，修復基金申請量激增。 工程師在最後安全檢查量到閘門向上移動。 重建勞動隊伍包括前士兵與難民，所有人都依同一套公開規則領薪。 妮米標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A message arrived after the public doors had closed. It carried no grand declaration, just a name and a time that did not match the official schedule. Liora resisted the urge to call it proof. Nemi opened a fresh page in the journal and recorded the discrepancy exactly. They had won time, not certainty, and there were still people who might lose everything if they hurried.

**繁中** 公共大門關閉後，一則訊息送到。它沒有豪言壯語，只有一個名字和一個對不上官方時程的時間。莉歐拉克制住把它當成證明的衝動。妮米翻開日誌新的一頁，逐字記錄差異。他們贏得的是時間，不是確定性；若急於求成，仍有人可能失去一切。

### 理解測驗

1. **Why does the first event matter?**
   - A. Liora opens a restitution office where people choose what memories they wish to recover or leave untouched.
   - B. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - C. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 莉歐拉設立補償辦公室，由當事人選擇想恢復或不願觸碰的記憶。

2. **Which finding changes the characters’ understanding?**
   - A. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - B. Nemi refuses to sing the old national hymn until the missing verses have names.
   - C. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 妮米堅持在缺失歌詞找到原主之前，不再演唱舊國歌。


## CHAPTER 24｜The City Without Orders｜不靠命令的城市

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：873**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The room smelled of dust, hot metal, and rain carried in on people's coats. Someone had left an ordinary object on the central table, yet no one wanted to touch it. A blizzard tests the new councils, whose volunteers keep the shelters running without compulsory miracles. Liora saw that the object mattered less for what it was than for the decision it proved had been made. There would be no honest way back to ignorance.

**繁中** 室內有灰塵、熱金屬與人們外套帶進的雨味。有人把平凡物件放在中央桌上，卻沒有人願意碰它。暴風雪考驗新議會，志工沒有強制奇蹟也維持避難所運作。莉歐拉明白重要的不是物件本身，而是它證明了何種決定已經發生。如今已不可能誠實地假裝無知。

**EN** There were children behind the temple steps who could remember the taste of bread but not the people who had taught them to bake it. Liora did not mistake their silence for gratitude. Every miracle that filled a bowl and emptied a memory left a different kind of hunger behind.

**繁中** 神殿階梯後方的孩子記得麵包滋味，卻不記得教自己烤麵包的人。莉歐拉沒有把沉默誤認為感激。每場填飽碗、卻掏空記憶的奇蹟，都會留下另一種飢餓。

**EN** Nemi arrived with a second account that neither confirmed nor dismissed the first. They compared the routes by which each record had reached the room, then placed the uncertainties beside the facts. Liora could feel the pressure to announce a solution before anyone became frightened. That pressure was precisely why a second witness mattered: a conclusion that could not survive an independent question would be dangerous, even if it happened to be right.

**繁中** 妮米帶來第二份說法，既未證實也未推翻第一份。兩人比對紀錄抵達房間的途徑，再把不確定之處和事實並列。莉歐拉感受到眾人希望立即宣布解方，以免恐懼擴大的壓力。但也正因這種壓力，第二位證人才重要：無法通過獨立質問的結論，即使碰巧正確，也可能很危險。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: Accordingly, each region would receive a published timetable for its first safety audit. A warning device began to beep during the public demonstration, and the operator stopped the test. The new chairperson published the full voting record instead of a private summary. Maelin rejected the idea that one kingdom had to civilize the others before their words deserved respect. A coastal cook served clam soup to delegates who had spent long hours arguing. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 因此，每個地區都將收到第一次安全稽核的公開時程。 公開示範時警示裝置開始發出嗶聲，操作員立刻停測。 新主席公布完整投票紀錄，而非私人摘要。 莉歐拉反對某國必須先教化別國，其語言才值得尊重的想法。 一名海岸廚師端出蛤蜊湯，供應爭論多時的代表。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The contradiction reached the table just as the meeting was about to end. When the storm ends, the people demand a regular recall procedure for every steward. For a long moment no one spoke. Liora understood why silence felt comfortable; each answer threatened someone's settled account of how the world worked. Nemi broke the stillness by asking who would be harmed if they simply filed the discovery away. No one could answer without naming a real person.

**繁中** 會議即將結束時，矛盾證據送上桌面。暴風雪結束後，民眾要求所有管理者都必須接受定期罷免。一時之間無人開口。莉歐拉明白沉默為何讓人舒服：任何答案都可能動搖某人對世界的既定理解。妮米打破沉默，問若把發現歸檔，究竟誰會受害。沒人能在不指名真實人物的情況下回答。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: A coalition of residents and workers proposed a permanent public safety council. A conscientious clerk recorded a complete transcript, and a simple bell gave one beep when each speaking period ended. The families gathered in a cozy schoolroom to read the new bilingual notices aloud. Hungry travelers began to devour the bread served after the lengthy assembly meeting. The report called the first outage disastrous without claiming that every later repair had failed. Liora asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 居民與工人聯盟提議設立常設公共安全委員會。 她用簡短聲明開場：任何社區都不能被排除在自身河流的決策之外。 家人聚集在溫暖舒適的教室，朗讀新的雙語公告。 飢餓旅客在漫長會議後大口吃起分發的麵包。 報告稱首次神殿沉默是災難，沒有宣稱後續所有修復都失敗。 莉歐拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Liora had expected the hardest part to be exposing the secret. Instead, it was choosing what to do after a partial truth became public. The people who had been ignored did not all want the same remedy. Nemi proposed that each affected group could submit a written objection and receive a specific answer. The method would take time, but it recognized them as participants rather than scenery.

**繁中** 莉歐拉原以為最難的是揭露祕密，後來才發現，部分真相公開後如何行動才是難題。曾被忽略的人並不全想要相同補救。妮米提議每個受影響群體都能提出書面異議並取得具體答覆。方法需要時間，但終於把他們當成參與者，而不是背景。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: Shareholders expressed disbelief when Lira refused special treatment for his own bid. The committee worked to formulate clear appeals rules before any contract was signed. Engineers from different latitudes explained why reservoirs near the northern mountains behaved differently from those in the warm tropic zones. Some delegates were patriotic and proud of their home territories; others feared that the word patriotism might again be used to silence criticism. The staff had to reckon with the cost of repairing the roof before reopening every gallery. Nemi marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 沉舟拒絕自家投標享有特殊待遇時，股東難以置信。 委員會在簽約前努力制定清楚的申訴規則。 不同緯度來的工程師解釋，北方山區的儲能庫與溫暖熱帶地區運作方式何以不同。 一名盡責書記記錄完整逐字稿，簡單的鈴聲在每段發言結束時發出一聲提示。 員工必須先計算屋頂修繕成本，才能開放所有展廳。 妮米標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** There were children behind the temple steps who could remember the taste of bread but not the people who had taught them to bake it. Liora did not mistake their silence for gratitude. Every miracle that filled a bowl and emptied a memory left a different kind of hunger behind.

**繁中** 神殿階梯後方的孩子記得麵包滋味，卻不記得教自己烤麵包的人。莉歐拉沒有把沉默誤認為感激。每場填飽碗、卻掏空記憶的奇蹟，都會留下另一種飢餓。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: A river's ripple was visible through the windows, small and constant beneath the grand language of peace. The director's stature in the industry did not exempt him from independent inspection. The court released a transcript of the hearing after removing protected witness addresses. A map of the northern tropic helped students compare ancient navigation systems. Farmers described how a change in water temperature could affect crops, fish, and even the growth of a vine along river walls. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 窗外河面泛起細小而持續的漣漪，在宏大的和平語言之下安靜流動。 董事在業界的地位不能使他免於獨立檢查。 法院刪除受保護證人地址後，公布聽證逐字稿。 北回歸線地圖協助學生比較古代導航系統。 農民描述水溫改變如何影響作物、魚類，甚至河牆上藤蔓的生長。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** When the lamps were lowered, Liora returned to a cracked voting bell. It was neither proof of innocence nor a shortcut to justice. It was a reminder that the next decision would be witnessed by people who no longer trusted easy promises. Nemi remained at the door while the final record was sealed. Outside, the twelve-bell capital waited for news that had to be more precise than a victory speech.

**繁中** 燈光調暗時，莉歐拉再次看向一口裂開的投票鐘。它既不是清白證明，也不是通往正義的捷徑，而是在提醒眾人：下一個決定會由不再相信輕易承諾的人見證。妮米守在門邊，最後一份紀錄被封存。外頭的十二鐘都等待的消息，必須比勝利演說更精確。

### 理解測驗

1. **What unexpected event begins this chapter?**
   - A. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - B. A blizzard tests the new councils, whose volunteers keep the shelters running without compulsory miracles.
   - C. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 暴風雪考驗新議會，志工沒有強制奇蹟也維持避難所運作。

2. **What does the later discovery establish?**
   - A. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - B. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   - C. When the storm ends, the people demand a regular recall procedure for every steward.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 暴風雪結束後，民眾要求所有管理者都必須接受定期罷免。


## CHAPTER 25｜The First Unbilled Dawn｜第一個不收費的黎明

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：854**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** Nobody had called it a crisis at first. A clerk had used a harmless word, a guard had followed an ordinary instruction, and by evening somebody else was carrying the cost. Liora refused to begin with the official conclusion. At dawn Liora hears the voting bell ring for a newborn whose name is freely entered into the register. In the twelve-bell capital, even an accurate number could become a lie when it left out the person whose life it counted.

**繁中** 起初沒有人稱這是危機。書記使用看似無害的詞，守衛照普通命令行事，到了晚上代價卻落在別人身上。莉歐拉拒絕從官方結論開始。黎明時，莉歐拉聽見投票鐘為一名自願登記姓名的新生兒響起。在十二鐘都，精確的數字若遺漏真正承受後果的人，也可能成為謊言。

**EN** The argument was not merely about who held authority. It was about what happened when an official decision met a household, a workplace, or a body that could not obey it safely. Liora asked Nemi to describe the smallest practical consequence. Only then did they realize that the proposed remedy would reproduce the very harm it claimed to prevent. They changed the plan before presenting it in public.

**繁中** 爭論不只是誰有權威，而是官方決定落到家庭、職場或無法安全服從的身體上時會發生什麼。莉歐拉請妮米說明最小、最實際的後果。他們這才發現提議中的補救會重複它聲稱要防止的傷害，因此在公開發表前修改了計畫。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The first spring after the peace charter arrived amid nervous anticipation. There was quiet anticipation before the first independent safety report was published. In the former military district, an apprentice engineer adjusted a heat valve while an experienced instructor checked the figures. The final report showed no evidence that anyone had tried to assassinate the city's executives. The old network had become a backbone of shared heating rather than a hidden weapon. Liora asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 和平憲章通過後的第一個春天，在緊張期待中到來。 第一份獨立安全報告公布前，現場有安靜的期待。 舊軍區裡，一名學徒工程師調整熱能閥門，資深教師核對數字。 最終報告沒有證據顯示有人試圖暗殺城市主管。 舊網路成了共同供暖的骨幹，不再是隱藏武器。 莉歐拉要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** What changed the discussion was a fact that should have been obvious from the start. She closes the old debt ledger, not because the damage has vanished, but because the future will no longer be charged in advance. Liora felt the old temptation to turn that fact into an immediate victory. Instead, the team separated what the new evidence established from what it merely suggested. That distinction frustrated the loudest spectators, but it helped the witnesses remain willing to speak again.

**繁中** 改變討論的是一件本來就該看得見的事實。她合上舊債帳，不是因為傷害已消失，而是未來不再提前向任何人收費。莉歐拉一瞬間也想把它當成立刻勝利的證明，團隊卻把新證據真正確立的部分與僅能推測的部分分開。這讓最喧鬧的旁觀者失望，卻使證人願意再次出面。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: A public board could compute energy use and publish it where any resident might read. The court found no corpse connected with the alleged language fraud, despite rumors spread to inflame the public. A witness using a crutch was given a chair near the entrance rather than being forced to stand. The false theft charge had caused a porter's detention before the evidence was reviewed. Families could dwell in the repaired neighborhood without fearing an unannounced transfer. Nemi marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 公共看板會計算能源使用量，張貼在任何居民都能閱讀的地方。 法庭確認語言造假指控與任何屍體無關，儘管有人散布流言煽動大眾。 一位拄拐杖的證人獲安排在入口附近坐下，沒有被迫站立。 虛假的失竊指控曾使搬運員在證據審閱前遭到拘留。 家庭可以住在修復後的社區，不必擔心未公告的轉權。 妮米標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** There were two tempting shortcuts. One would protect the reputation of Chancellor Varos and leave the public guessing. The other would expose every private detail at once and call that transparency. Liora rejected both. The issue was whether a miracle may be taken from a citizen without consent, not whether the team could win an argument in a single afternoon. A smaller step, supported by independent observers, could protect people while the wider dispute remained open.

**繁中** 有兩種誘人的捷徑：一種保住瓦洛斯宰相的名聲，讓大眾自行猜測；另一種把所有私人細節一次揭露，並稱之為透明。莉歐拉兩者都拒絕。核心是奇蹟能否未經同意就向公民索取代價，不是團隊能否在一個下午贏得爭論。由獨立觀察者支持的一小步，即使爭議未結，也能先保護民眾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The office became frantic when several journalists demanded the verdict before it was issued. Children ran past a small hostel that now housed visitors from both territories. Halda was a knowledgeable navigator, yet she still invited younger keepers to challenge her choices. The safety officer closed a route when the engineer identified a potentially lethal collapse risk. A lizard warmed itself on a stone wall, and for a moment the street seemed wonderfully ordinary. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 數名記者要求提前知道判決時，辦公室一片慌亂。 孩子們跑過一間小旅舍，如今裡面住著兩地旅人。 哈爾妲是知識豐富的引航者，仍邀年輕保管人質疑她的選擇。 工程師指出可能致命的坍塌風險後，安全員封閉路線。 一隻蜥蜴在石牆上曬太陽，整條街忽然顯得平凡得美好。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The remainder of the compensation fund stayed under independent public supervision. The judge's careful finding could stun the public without turning justice into entertainment. Successive inspections would continue after Lira resigned from the executive position. The treasury was ordered to return the diverted printing funds and publish a separate account of compensation. Maelin felt a slight vibration through the wooden desk as the final register was closed. A missing vowel in an old family name had once disguised the identity of a rightful lender. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 賠償基金剩餘款項仍由獨立公共機制監督。 法官謹慎的裁定可能震驚公眾，但不會把正義變成娛樂。 莉拉辭去執行長後，連續的檢查仍將繼續。 國庫被命令返還挪用的印製款，並另行公布補償帳目。 最後登記冊闔上時，莉歐拉感到木桌傳來輕微震動。 舊家族姓氏少了一個母音，曾使真正出借者的身分被掩蓋。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The chapter ended without an easy reassurance. Liora had learned one thing that could be checked and another that still needed an answer. Nemi wrote both on the same page so that relief would not erase doubt. Their decision had moved the crisis forward, but it had also drawn the attention of Chancellor Varos. By morning, the next question would reach people far beyond this room.

**繁中** 這一天沒有簡單的安慰。莉歐拉知道了一件能查證的事，也留下另一件尚待回答的事。妮米把兩者寫在同一頁，免得鬆一口氣就抹掉疑問。他們的決定使危機向前，也引起瓦洛斯宰相注意。明早下一個問題將傳到房間外更遠的地方。

**EN** Liora did not call the day a perfect victory. She closes the old debt ledger, not because the damage has vanished, but because the future will no longer be charged in advance. What endured was a decision that the people of the twelve-bell capital could challenge, revise, and make their own. Nemi placed a cracked voting bell beside the public record. They left the last page open for names not yet written.

**繁中** 莉歐拉不把這一天稱為完美勝利。她合上舊債帳，不是因為傷害已消失，而是未來不再提前向任何人收費。真正留下的是十二鐘都人民有權質疑、修改並共同擁有的決定。妮米把一口裂開的投票鐘放在公共紀錄旁。他們把最後一頁留白，等待未來尚未寫上的姓名。

### 理解測驗

1. **What important problem does the opening scene reveal?**
   - A. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - B. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - C. At dawn Liora hears the voting bell ring for a newborn whose name is freely entered into the register.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 黎明時，莉歐拉聽見投票鐘為一名自願登記姓名的新生兒響起。

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. She closes the old debt ledger, not because the damage has vanished, but because the future will no longer be charged in advance.
   - B. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - C. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 她合上舊債帳，不是因為傷害已消失，而是未來不再提前向任何人收費。
